# embedded: 오백종목_섹터에너지_알파하이킨아시_통합_V1_0.py
COPY <<'ALPHA500_E89588D5222C' /app/오백종목_섹터에너지_알파하이킨아시_통합_V1_0.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
오백종목_섹터에너지_알파하이킨아시_통합_V1_0.py

실행 파이프라인
==============
LS KRX master/ranking
 -> 500 Universe (20일 거래대금 고유동성 100 강제편입)
 -> 500종목 경량 Stage1 (Alpha Heikin-Ashi)
 -> KOSPI/KOSDAQ 실시간 지수 + 5D/20D 비대칭 RS
 -> 133개 내외 ENERGY_CLUSTER (163 MICRO_CLUSTER의 소표본 fallback)
 -> Strong-sector lane 70% + Independent-RS rescue lane 30%
 -> Stage2 70개
 -> LS Deep 30개 (체결강도/외인기관/프로그램)
 -> 최종 0~10개 (조건 미달 시 강제충원 없음)

속도 원칙
=========
- 최초 history cache가 없는 종목만 t8410 일봉을 1회 bootstrap한다.
- 반복 장중 스캔은 t8407 멀티현재가(50종목/호출)로 당일 봉을 갱신한다.
- KRX/NXT를 절대 혼합하지 않는다.
- 주문 API를 사용하지 않는다.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import logging
import math
import os
import time
from collections import defaultdict
from datetime import date, datetime, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Mapping, Optional, Sequence, Tuple

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from 오백종목_유동성군집_선정엔진_V1_0 import (
    AdaptiveLSProvider,
    Universe500Selector,
    env_float,
    env_int,
    fnum,
)
from 전체시장_2단계_딥스캔_V1_1 import (

    LSDeepProvider,
    TwoStageDeepScanner,
)
from 전체시장_알파하이킨아시_KIS_LS_스캐너_V1_0 import BrokerError, HistoryStore, SymbolInfo

LOG = logging.getLogger("alpha500_integrated")
logging.basicConfig(level=os.getenv("LOG_LEVEL", "INFO"), format="%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(message)s")
KST = timezone(timedelta(hours=9))

def kst_now() -> datetime:
    return datetime.now(KST)

def market_session_now(now: Optional[datetime] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """KST 기준 자동 거래소 라우터. KRX/NXT 가격·캐시를 절대 혼합하지 않는다."""
    now = now or kst_now()
    hm = now.hour * 60 + now.minute + now.second / 60.0
    weekday = now.weekday() < 5
    if not weekday:
        return {"session": "CLOSED", "venue": "NXT", "exchgubun": "N", "scan_allowed": False}
    if 8 * 60 <= hm < 8 * 60 + 50:
        return {"session": "NXT_PRE", "venue": "NXT", "exchgubun": "N", "scan_allowed": True}
    if 8 * 60 + 50 <= hm < 9 * 60:
        return {"session": "NXT_PRE_CLOSE", "venue": "NXT", "exchgubun": "N", "scan_allowed": False}
    if 9 * 60 <= hm < 15 * 60 + 30:
        return {"session": "KRX_REGULAR", "venue": "KRX", "exchgubun": "K", "scan_allowed": True}
    if 15 * 60 + 30 <= hm < 15 * 60 + 40:
        return {"session": "KRX_CLOSE", "venue": "KRX", "exchgubun": "K", "scan_allowed": False}
    if 15 * 60 + 40 <= hm < 20 * 60:
        return {"session": "NXT_AFTER", "venue": "NXT", "exchgubun": "N", "scan_allowed": True}
    return {"session": "WAITING_NXT", "venue": "NXT", "exchgubun": "N", "scan_allowed": False}

def write_json_atomic(path: Path, obj: Any):
    tmp = path.with_suffix(path.suffix + ".tmp")
    tmp.write_text(json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default=str), encoding="utf-8")
    tmp.replace(path)



def clip100(v: float) -> float:
    return float(np.clip(v, 0.0, 100.0))


def norm(v: float, lo: float, hi: float) -> float:
    if not math.isfinite(v) or hi <= lo:
        return 0.5
    return float(np.clip((v - lo) / (hi - lo), 0.0, 1.0))


class LSIndexProvider:
    INDEX_CODE = {"KOSPI": "001", "KOSDAQ": "301"}

    def __init__(self, ls: AdaptiveLSProvider):
        self.ls = ls

    def current(self, market: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        upcode = self.INDEX_CODE[market]
        j = self.ls._post_tr(
            "/indtp/market-data", "t1511", {"t1511InBlock": {"upcode": upcode}}
        ).json()
        z = j.get("t1511OutBlock") or {}
        return {
            "market": market,
            "code": upcode,
            "name": str(z.get("hname", market)),
            "index": fnum(z.get("pricejisu"), float("nan")),
            "prev_index": fnum(z.get("jniljisu"), float("nan")),
            "return_pct": fnum(z.get("diffjisu"), float("nan")),
            "volume": fnum(z.get("volume"), float("nan")),
            "value": fnum(z.get("value"), float("nan")),
        }

    def daily(self, market: str, count: int = 80) -> pd.DataFrame:
        shcode = self.INDEX_CODE[market]
        end = date.today()
        start = end - timedelta(days=max(150, count * 2))
        body = {
            "t8429InBlock": {
                "shcode": shcode,
                "gubun": "2",
                "qrycnt": max(40, count),
                "sdate": start.strftime("%Y%m%d"),
                "edate": end.strftime("%Y%m%d"),
                "cts_date": "",
                "comp_yn": "N",
            }
        }
        j = self.ls._post_tr("/indtp/chart", "t8429", body).json()
        rows = j.get("t8429OutBlock1") or []
        out = []
        for z in rows:
            try:
                out.append({
                    "timestamp": pd.to_datetime(str(z.get("date")), format="%Y%m%d"),
                    "open": fnum(z.get("open")), "high": fnum(z.get("high")),
                    "low": fnum(z.get("low")), "close": fnum(z.get("close")),
                    "volume": fnum(z.get("jdiff_vol")), "turnover": fnum(z.get("value")),
                })
            except Exception:
                continue
        df = pd.DataFrame(out)
        if df.empty:
            raise BrokerError(f"LS t8429 {market} empty")
        return df.sort_values("timestamp").drop_duplicates("timestamp").tail(count).reset_index(drop=True)


class FastHistory500:
    """거래소별 일봉 cache + 현재가 overlay. KRX와 NXT 캐시는 venue key로 완전 분리한다."""

    def __init__(self, ls: AdaptiveLSProvider, db_path: str, min_bars: int = 70, lookback_days: int = 220):
        self.ls = ls
        self.store = HistoryStore(db_path)
        self.min_bars = min_bars
        self.lookback_days = lookback_days
        self.provider_name = "LS"
        self.venue = "KRX"
        self.exchgubun = "K"

    def set_venue(self, venue: str, exchgubun: Optional[str] = None):
        v = str(venue or "KRX").upper()
        if v not in {"KRX", "NXT"}:
            raise ValueError(f"unsupported venue={v}")
        self.venue = v
        self.exchgubun = exchgubun or ("N" if v == "NXT" else "K")

    def multi_quotes(self, codes: Sequence[str]) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]:
        out: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
        codes = [str(x).zfill(6) for x in codes]
        if self.venue == "KRX":
            for i in range(0, len(codes), 50):
                chunk = codes[i:i + 50]
                body = {"t8407InBlock": {"nrec": len(chunk), "shcode": "".join(chunk)}}
                j = self.ls._post_tr("/stock/market-data", "t8407", body).json()
                for z in j.get("t8407OutBlock1") or []:
                    code = str(z.get("shcode", "")).strip().zfill(6)
                    if code:
                        out[code] = z
            return out

        # NXT: 통합 현재가 TR t8450을 거래소 N으로 명시. 종목별 10/sec 제한을 준수한다.
        for i, code in enumerate(codes, 1):
            try:
                body = {"t8450InBlock": {"shcode": code, "exchgubun": self.exchgubun}}
                j = self.ls._post_tr("/stock/market-data", "t8450", body).json()
                z = j.get("t8450OutBlock") or {}
                if z and fnum(z.get("price"), 0) > 0:
                    z = dict(z); z["shcode"] = code
                    out[code] = z
            except Exception as e:
                LOG.debug("NXT quote %s skipped: %s", code, e)
            if i % 100 == 0:
                LOG.info("NXT QUOTES %d/%d", i, len(codes))
        return out

    def _needs_history_refresh(self, code: str) -> bool:
        cached = self.store.read(self.provider_name, self.venue, code, limit=self.min_bars + 10)
        if len(cached) < self.min_bars:
            return True
        last = self.store.last_date(self.provider_name, self.venue, code)
        if last is None:
            return True
        return (date.today() - last).days > env_int("HISTORY_REFRESH_GAP_DAYS", 4)

    def bootstrap_one(self, code: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
        start = date.today() - timedelta(days=self.lookback_days)
        if self.venue == "KRX":
            df = self.ls.get_daily_bars(code, start, date.today())
        else:
            body = {
                "t8451InBlock": {
                    "shcode": code, "gubun": "2", "qrycnt": 500,
                    "sdate": start.strftime("%Y%m%d"), "edate": date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d"),
                    "cts_date": "", "comp_yn": "N", "sujung": "Y", "exchgubun": self.exchgubun,
                }
            }
            j = self.ls._post_tr(self.ls.chart_path, "t8451", body).json()
            rows = j.get("t8451OutBlock1") or []
            out = []
            for z in rows:
                try:
                    out.append({
                        "timestamp": pd.to_datetime(str(z.get("date")), format="%Y%m%d"),
                        "open": fnum(z.get("open")), "high": fnum(z.get("high")),
                        "low": fnum(z.get("low")), "close": fnum(z.get("close")),
                        "volume": fnum(z.get("jdiff_vol", z.get("volume"))),
                        "turnover": fnum(z.get("value", z.get("volume_amt"))),
                    })
                except Exception:
                    continue
            df = pd.DataFrame(out)
            if df.empty:
                raise BrokerError(f"LS {code} t8451 NXT empty")
            df = df.sort_values("timestamp").drop_duplicates("timestamp").reset_index(drop=True)
        self.store.upsert(self.provider_name, self.venue, code, df)
        return self.store.read(self.provider_name, self.venue, code, limit=240)

    @staticmethod
    def overlay_quote(cached: pd.DataFrame, q: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> pd.DataFrame:
        if q is None:
            return cached
        price = fnum(q.get("price"), 0)
        prev = fnum(q.get("jnilclose"), 0)
        op = fnum(q.get("open"), price or prev)
        hi = fnum(q.get("high"), max(price, op) if price and op else 0)
        lo = fnum(q.get("low"), min(price, op) if price and op else 0)
        vol = fnum(q.get("volume"), 0)
        if price <= 0:
            return cached
        if op <= 0: op = price
        if hi <= 0: hi = max(price, op)
        if lo <= 0: lo = min(price, op)
        today = pd.Timestamp(date.today())
        row = pd.DataFrame([{
            "timestamp": today, "open": op, "high": hi, "low": lo, "close": price,
            "volume": vol, "turnover": fnum(q.get("value", q.get("volume_amt")), 0),
        }])
        x = cached.copy()
        if not x.empty:
            x = x[pd.to_datetime(x["timestamp"]).dt.date != date.today()]
        x = pd.concat([x, row], ignore_index=True)
        return x.sort_values("timestamp").tail(240).reset_index(drop=True)

    def get_all(self, universe: pd.DataFrame, bootstrap_missing: bool = True) -> Tuple[Dict[str, pd.DataFrame], Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]], List[str]]:
        codes = universe["code"].astype(str).str.zfill(6).tolist()
        quotes = self.multi_quotes(codes)
        data: Dict[str, pd.DataFrame] = {}
        errors: List[str] = []
        missing = [c for c in codes if self._needs_history_refresh(c)]
        if missing:
            LOG.info("history bootstrap/refresh required=%d venue=%s", len(missing), self.venue)
        for i, code in enumerate(missing, 1):
            if not bootstrap_missing:
                continue
            try:
                self.bootstrap_one(code)
            except Exception as e:
                errors.append(f"{code}:{type(e).__name__}:{str(e)[:100]}")
            if i % 25 == 0 or i == len(missing):
                LOG.info("history bootstrap %d/%d venue=%s", i, len(missing), self.venue)

        for code in codes:
            cached = self.store.read(self.provider_name, self.venue, code, limit=240)
            if len(cached) < self.min_bars:
                errors.append(f"{code}:bars={len(cached)}:{self.venue}")
                continue
            data[code] = self.overlay_quote(cached, quotes.get(code))
        return data, quotes, errors


class Alpha500Integrated(TwoStageDeepScanner):
    def __init__(self, master_csv: str):
        os.environ["SCAN_PROVIDER"] = "ls"
        os.environ["DEEP_PROVIDER"] = "ls"
        os.environ["VENUE"] = "KRX"
        super().__init__()
        self.master_csv = master_csv
        self.stage2_n = env_int("STAGE2_CANDIDATES", 70)
        self.deep_n = env_int("DEEP_CANDIDATES", 30)
        self.final_top_n = env_int("FINAL_TOP_N", 10)
        self.stage1_min_score = env_float("STAGE1_MIN_SCORE", 46.0)
        self.ls = AdaptiveLSProvider()
        # Market/Deep 양쪽이 동일 LS read-only provider를 공유한다.
        self.market.ls = self.ls
        self.market.provider_mode = "ls"
        self.deep = CompositeDeepProvider(
            LSDeepProvider(self.ls),
            KISDeepProvider(self.market.kis),
            "ls",
        )
        self.selector = Universe500Selector(master_csv, ls=self.ls)
        self.index = LSIndexProvider(self.ls)
        db = os.getenv("CACHE_DB", str(Path(os.getenv("ALPHA500_DATA_DIR", "./data")) / "alpha500_bars.sqlite3"))
        self.fast_history = FastHistory500(
            self.ls, db_path=db,
            min_bars=env_int("MIN_BARS", 70),
            lookback_days=env_int("LOOKBACK_DAYS", 220),
        )
        self.result_dir = Path(os.getenv("RESULT_DIR", "./results"))
        self.result_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.session_info = {"session": "KRX_REGULAR", "venue": "KRX", "exchgubun": "K", "scan_allowed": True}
        self.active_venue = "KRX"
        self.active_exchgubun = "K"

    def set_market_session(self, info: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]] = None):
        info = dict(info or market_session_now())
        venue = str(info.get("venue", "KRX")).upper()
        exchgubun = str(info.get("exchgubun", "N" if venue == "NXT" else "K"))
        self.session_info = info
        self.active_venue = venue
        self.active_exchgubun = exchgubun
        self.fast_history.set_venue(venue, exchgubun)
        if hasattr(self.selector, "set_exchange"):
            self.selector.set_exchange(exchgubun)
        if hasattr(self.deep, "ls") and hasattr(self.deep.ls, "set_exchange"):
            self.deep.ls.set_exchange(exchgubun, venue)
        os.environ["LS_ACTIVE_EXCHGUBUN"] = exchgubun
        os.environ["ACTIVE_VENUE"] = venue
        return info

    def run_stage1_fast(self, runtime: pd.DataFrame, bootstrap_missing: bool = True) -> Tuple[pd.DataFrame, Dict[str, pd.DataFrame], Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]]:
        data_cache, quotes, errs = self.fast_history.get_all(runtime, bootstrap_missing=bootstrap_missing)
        meta = runtime.set_index("code").to_dict("index")
        rows = []
        for i, code in enumerate(runtime["code"].astype(str).str.zfill(6), 1):
            m = meta.get(code, {})
            bars = data_cache.get(code)
            if bars is None or bars.empty:
                rows.append({
                    "symbol": code, "name": m.get("name", code), "market": m.get("market", "KRX"),
                    "stage1_state": "ERROR", "stage1_score": 0.0, "current_return_pct": np.nan,
                    "error": "history unavailable",
                })
                continue
            try:
                d = self.stage1_pre_score(bars, code)
                q = quotes.get(code, {})
                # t8407의 등락률을 당일 실시간 우선값으로 사용한다.
                if q and q.get("diff") not in (None, ""):
                    d["current_return_pct"] = fnum(q.get("diff"), d.get("current_return_pct", 0.0))
                d.update({
                    "name": m.get("name", code), "market": m.get("market", "KRX"),
                    "stage1_price_source": ("LS:t8407+t8410_cache:KRX" if self.active_venue == "KRX" else "LS:t8450+t8451_cache:NXT"),
                    "stage1_fallback_trace": "",
                    "trade_strength_snapshot": fnum(q.get("chdegree"), np.nan) if q else np.nan,
                    "turnover_snapshot_million": fnum(q.get("value"), np.nan) if q else np.nan,
                })
                # runtime metadata
                for c in [
                    "BUCKET_BASE","MEGA_SECTOR","SECTOR","MICRO_CLUSTER","ENERGY_CLUSTER","ENERGY_LEVEL",
                    "SECONDARY_TAG","역할","에너지기본가중","매수후보","시총1000억필터","활성상태",
                    "LIQUIDITY_ANCHOR","liquidity_score","liquidity_rank","market_cap_100m","ENERGY_EXCLUDE","nxt_eligible",
                ]:
                    d[c] = m.get(c, "")
                rows.append(d)
            except Exception as e:
                rows.append({
                    "symbol": code, "name": m.get("name", code), "market": m.get("market", "KRX"),
                    "stage1_state": "ERROR", "stage1_score": 0.0, "current_return_pct": np.nan,
                    "error": str(e)[:240], "ENERGY_CLUSTER": m.get("ENERGY_CLUSTER", ""),
                    "ENERGY_EXCLUDE": m.get("ENERGY_EXCLUDE", "N"),
                })
            if i % 100 == 0:
                LOG.info("FAST STAGE1 %d/%d", i, len(runtime))
        out = pd.DataFrame(rows)
        return out, data_cache, quotes

    @staticmethod
    def _aligned_returns(stock: pd.DataFrame, index: pd.DataFrame, n: int = 20) -> pd.DataFrame:
        s = stock[["timestamp", "close"]].copy().rename(columns={"close": "sclose"})
        b = index[["timestamp", "close"]].copy().rename(columns={"close": "bclose"})
        s["timestamp"] = pd.to_datetime(s["timestamp"]).dt.normalize()
        b["timestamp"] = pd.to_datetime(b["timestamp"]).dt.normalize()
        x = pd.merge(s, b, on="timestamp", how="inner").sort_values("timestamp")
        x["sr"] = x["sclose"].pct_change()
        x["br"] = x["bclose"].pct_change()
        return x.dropna().tail(max(n, 25))

    @staticmethod
    def _capture_metrics(x: pd.DataFrame) -> Tuple[Optional[float], Optional[float]]:
        if x.empty:
            return None, None
        up = x[x["br"] > 0]
        dn = x[x["br"] < 0]
        upcap = None
        downcap = None
        if len(up) >= 3 and abs(up["br"].mean()) > 1e-9:
            upcap = float(up["sr"].mean() / up["br"].mean())
        if len(dn) >= 3 and abs(dn["br"].mean()) > 1e-9:
            downcap = float(abs(dn["sr"].mean()) / abs(dn["br"].mean()))
        return upcap, downcap

    @staticmethod
    def _asym_class(upcap: Optional[float], downcap: Optional[float]) -> str:
        if upcap is None or downcap is None:
            return "NEUTRAL"
        if downcap <= 0.50 and upcap >= 2.00:
            return "SUPER"
        if downcap <= 0.65 and upcap >= 1.50:
            return "STRONG"
        if downcap <= 0.80 and upcap >= 1.20:
            return "POSITIVE"
        if downcap > 1.10 and upcap < 0.90:
            return "WEAK"
        return "NEUTRAL"

    @staticmethod
    def _cum_return_pct(x: pd.Series, n: int) -> float:
        y = x.dropna().tail(n)
        if y.empty:
            return float("nan")
        return float(((1.0 + y).prod() - 1.0) * 100.0)

    def attach_market_rs(self, stage1: pd.DataFrame, data_cache: Dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> Tuple[pd.DataFrame, Dict[str, Any]]:
        # NXT는 KRX 지수를 섞지 않고 NXT 대상종목의 시장별 중앙값을 동시간 benchmark로 사용한다.
        if self.active_venue == "NXT":
            x = stage1.copy()
            med = x.groupby("market")["current_return_pct"].median().to_dict() if not x.empty else {}
            rows = []
            for _, r in x.iterrows():
                d = dict(r)
                bench = fnum(med.get(str(r.get("market"))), 0.0)
                stock_ret = fnum(r.get("current_return_pct"), float("nan"))
                d["index_return_pct"] = bench
                d["market_rs"] = stock_ret - bench if math.isfinite(stock_ret) else np.nan
                d["rs5"] = np.nan; d["rs20"] = np.nan
                d["up_capture20"] = np.nan; d["down_capture20"] = np.nan
                d["asym_rs_class"] = "NEUTRAL"
                d["market_rs_score"] = clip100(50.0 + np.clip(fnum(d.get("market_rs"), 0.0) * 12.0, -30, 30))
                rows.append(d)
            ctx = {"current": {m: {"market": m, "return_pct": float(v), "source": "NXT_CROSS_SECTION_MEDIAN"} for m,v in med.items()}, "history": {}}
            return pd.DataFrame(rows), ctx

        idx_cur: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
        idx_hist: Dict[str, pd.DataFrame] = {}
        for m in ("KOSPI", "KOSDAQ"):
            try:
                idx_cur[m] = self.index.current(m)
                idx_hist[m] = self.index.daily(m, 80)
            except Exception as e:
                LOG.warning("index %s unavailable: %s", m, e)
                idx_cur[m] = {"market": m, "return_pct": float("nan"), "error": str(e)}
                idx_hist[m] = pd.DataFrame()

        rows = []
        for _, r in stage1.iterrows():
            d = dict(r)
            market = str(r.get("market", ""))
            bench = fnum(idx_cur.get(market, {}).get("return_pct"), float("nan"))
            stock_ret = fnum(r.get("current_return_pct"), float("nan"))
            d["index_return_pct"] = bench
            d["market_rs"] = stock_ret - bench if math.isfinite(stock_ret) and math.isfinite(bench) else np.nan
            d["rs5"] = np.nan
            d["rs20"] = np.nan
            d["up_capture20"] = np.nan
            d["down_capture20"] = np.nan
            d["asym_rs_class"] = "NEUTRAL"
            bars = data_cache.get(str(r.get("symbol")))
            ih = idx_hist.get(market)
            if bars is not None and ih is not None and not ih.empty:
                x = self._aligned_returns(bars, ih, 22)
                if not x.empty:
                    d["rs5"] = self._cum_return_pct(x["sr"], 5) - self._cum_return_pct(x["br"], 5)
                    d["rs20"] = self._cum_return_pct(x["sr"], 20) - self._cum_return_pct(x["br"], 20)
                    up, dn = self._capture_metrics(x.tail(20))
                    d["up_capture20"] = up if up is not None else np.nan
                    d["down_capture20"] = dn if dn is not None else np.nan
                    d["asym_rs_class"] = self._asym_class(up, dn)
            cls_bonus = {"SUPER": 15, "STRONG": 10, "POSITIVE": 5, "NEUTRAL": 0, "WEAK": -8}.get(d["asym_rs_class"], 0)
            score = 50.0
            if math.isfinite(fnum(d.get("market_rs"), float("nan"))):
                score += np.clip(fnum(d.get("market_rs")) * 10.0, -25, 25)
            if math.isfinite(fnum(d.get("rs5"), float("nan"))):
                score += np.clip(fnum(d.get("rs5")) * 2.0, -12, 12)
            if math.isfinite(fnum(d.get("rs20"), float("nan"))):
                score += np.clip(fnum(d.get("rs20")) * 0.8, -10, 10)
            d["market_rs_score"] = clip100(score + cls_bonus)
            rows.append(d)
        return pd.DataFrame(rows), {"current": idx_cur, "history": idx_hist}

    @staticmethod
    def turnover_accel(bars: Optional[pd.DataFrame]) -> float:
        if bars is None or len(bars) < 8 or "turnover" not in bars.columns:
            return float("nan")
        x = pd.to_numeric(bars["turnover"], errors="coerce").dropna()
        if len(x) < 8:
            return float("nan")
        base = x.iloc[-21:-1].mean() if len(x) >= 21 else x.iloc[:-1].mean()
        return float(x.iloc[-1] / base) if base and base > 0 else float("nan")

    def calculate_cluster_energy(self, stage1: pd.DataFrame, data_cache: Dict[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> Tuple[pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame]:
        x = stage1.copy()
        x["turnover_accel"] = x["symbol"].map(lambda s: self.turnover_accel(data_cache.get(str(s))))
        valid = x[
            (x.get("ENERGY_EXCLUDE", "N").astype(str) != "Y") &
            (x["stage1_state"] != "ERROR") &
            x["ENERGY_CLUSTER"].astype(str).ne("")
        ].copy()
        rows = []
        for cluster, g in valid.groupby("ENERGY_CLUSTER"):
            mrs = pd.to_numeric(g["market_rs"], errors="coerce")
            ret = pd.to_numeric(g["current_return_pct"], errors="coerce")
            ta = pd.to_numeric(g["turnover_accel"], errors="coerce")
            hold = (
                g["stage1_above_vwap"].fillna(False).astype(bool) &
                g["stage1_ema20_hold"].fillna(False).astype(bool)
            ).mean() if len(g) else 0.0
            breadth = float((mrs > 0).mean()) if mrs.notna().any() else 0.0
            mean_mrs = float(mrs.mean()) if mrs.notna().any() else 0.0
            avg_ret = float(ret.mean()) if ret.notna().any() else 0.0
            turn = float(ta.replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan).median()) if ta.notna().any() else 1.0

            cap = pd.to_numeric(g.get("market_cap_100m"), errors="coerce") if "market_cap_100m" in g.columns else pd.Series(dtype=float)
            if len(cap) and cap.notna().sum() >= 2:
                w = np.sqrt(cap.clip(lower=1).fillna(cap.median()))
                power_mrs = float(np.average(mrs.fillna(mean_mrs), weights=w))
                cap_coverage = float(cap.notna().mean())
            else:
                power_mrs = mean_mrs
                cap_coverage = 0.0

            rotation = (
                35 * norm(mean_mrs, -1.5, 2.0)
                + 25 * breadth
                + 20 * norm(turn, 0.70, 1.80)
                + 20 * hold
            )
            power = (
                60 * norm(power_mrs, -1.5, 2.0)
                + 20 * breadth
                + 20 * norm(turn, 0.70, 1.80)
            )
            energy = clip100(0.65 * rotation + 0.35 * power)
            if mean_mrs < -1.0 and breadth < 0.30:
                state = "S5"
            elif energy >= 82 and (avg_ret >= 4.5 or turn >= 2.20):
                state = "S4"
            elif energy >= 70:
                state = "S3"
            elif energy >= 58:
                state = "S2"
            elif energy >= 45:
                state = "S1"
            else:
                state = "S0"
            rows.append({
                "ENERGY_CLUSTER": cluster,
                "cluster_members": int(len(g)),
                "cluster_breadth": round(breadth, 4),
                "cluster_avg_return": round(avg_ret, 4),
                "cluster_market_rs": round(mean_mrs, 4),
                "cluster_turnover_accel": round(turn, 4),
                "cluster_hold_ratio": round(float(hold), 4),
                "cluster_power": round(power, 2),
                "cluster_rotation": round(rotation, 2),
                "cluster_energy": round(energy, 2),
                "cluster_state": state,
                "cap_coverage": round(cap_coverage, 3),
            })
        cdf = pd.DataFrame(rows)
        if cdf.empty:
            for c in ["cluster_members","cluster_breadth","cluster_avg_return","cluster_market_rs","cluster_turnover_accel","cluster_hold_ratio","cluster_power","cluster_rotation","cluster_energy","cluster_state","cap_coverage"]:
                x[c] = np.nan
            return x, cdf
        x = x.merge(cdf, on="ENERGY_CLUSTER", how="left")
        x["cluster_rs"] = pd.to_numeric(x["current_return_pct"], errors="coerce") - pd.to_numeric(x["cluster_avg_return"], errors="coerce")
        return x, cdf.sort_values(["cluster_energy", "cluster_rotation"], ascending=False).reset_index(drop=True)

    def attach_integrated_score(self, stage1: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
        x = stage1.copy()
        scores = []
        for _, r in x.iterrows():
            s1 = fnum(r.get("stage1_score"), 0.0)
            energy = fnum(r.get("cluster_energy"), 45.0)
            mrs = fnum(r.get("market_rs_score"), 50.0)
            crs = 50 + np.clip(fnum(r.get("cluster_rs"), 0.0) * 10.0, -25, 25)
            asym = {"SUPER": 95, "STRONG": 85, "POSITIVE": 70, "NEUTRAL": 50, "WEAK": 25}.get(str(r.get("asym_rs_class")), 50)
            score = 0.45 * s1 + 0.20 * energy + 0.15 * mrs + 0.10 * crs + 0.10 * asym
            if str(r.get("cluster_state")) == "S4":
                score -= 7.0
            if str(r.get("cluster_state")) == "S5":
                score -= 12.0
            # 이미 크게 발산한 날 신규 추격을 억제
            if fnum(r.get("current_return_pct"), 0.0) > env_float("MAX_FRESH_RETURN_PCT", 7.0):
                score -= min(15.0, (fnum(r.get("current_return_pct")) - 7.0) * 2.5)
            scores.append(clip100(score))
        x["stage1_score_raw"] = x["stage1_score"]
        x["integrated_score"] = scores
        # 기존 Deep scorer가 이 점수를 구조점수로 사용하도록 stage1_score를 통합점수로 교체한다.
        x["stage1_score"] = x["integrated_score"]
        return x

    def select_stage2(self, stage1: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
        valid = stage1[
            stage1["stage1_state"].isin(["UP_START", "REVERSAL_ARMED", "DOWN_WEAKEN", "UP_TREND"])
        ].copy()
        valid = valid[pd.to_numeric(valid["stage1_score_raw"], errors="coerce") >= self.stage1_min_score]
        if valid.empty:
            return valid

        strong = valid[valid["cluster_state"].isin(["S2", "S3"])].copy()
        rescue = valid[
            valid["asym_rs_class"].isin(["SUPER", "STRONG"]) |
            (pd.to_numeric(valid["market_rs"], errors="coerce") >= 2.0) |
            (pd.to_numeric(valid["rs5"], errors="coerce") >= 4.0)
        ].copy()
        strong = strong.sort_values(["integrated_score", "cluster_energy", "market_rs_score"], ascending=False)
        rescue = rescue.sort_values(["integrated_score", "market_rs_score", "rs5"], ascending=False)
        n_strong = int(round(self.stage2_n * 0.70))
        chosen = pd.concat([strong.head(n_strong), rescue.head(self.stage2_n - n_strong)], ignore_index=True)
        chosen = chosen.drop_duplicates("symbol", keep="first")
        if len(chosen) < self.stage2_n:
            rest = valid[~valid["symbol"].isin(chosen["symbol"])].sort_values("integrated_score", ascending=False)
            chosen = pd.concat([chosen, rest.head(self.stage2_n - len(chosen))], ignore_index=True)
        return chosen.head(self.stage2_n).reset_index(drop=True)

    def select_deep30(self, stage2: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
        if stage2.empty:
            return stage2
        x = stage2.copy()
        # 과열 S4는 독립 RS SUPER/STRONG일 때만 Deep 허용
        allow = (x["cluster_state"] != "S4") | x["asym_rs_class"].isin(["SUPER", "STRONG"])
        x = x[allow]
        return x.sort_values(["integrated_score", "market_rs_score", "cluster_energy"], ascending=False).head(self.deep_n).reset_index(drop=True)

    def run_deep_custom(self, candidates: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
        if candidates.empty:
            return candidates
        rows = []
        for i, (_, r) in enumerate(candidates.iterrows(), 1):
            code = str(r["symbol"]).zfill(6)
            d: DeepMetrics = self.deep.collect(code)
            d.sector_code = str(r.get("ENERGY_CLUSTER", ""))
            d.sector_breadth = fnum(r.get("cluster_breadth"), None) if not pd.isna(r.get("cluster_breadth")) else None
            d.sector_avg_return = fnum(r.get("cluster_avg_return"), None) if not pd.isna(r.get("cluster_avg_return")) else None
            d.sector_member_count = int(fnum(r.get("cluster_members"), 0)) or None
            d.source_sector = "CUSTOM_500_ENERGY_CLUSTER"
            score, comp, reasons = self.final_score(r, d)
            row = dict(r)
            row.update(d.to_dict())
            row.update(comp)
            row["deep_score"] = round(score, 2)
            row["deep_reasons"] = " | ".join(reasons)
            rows.append(row)
            LOG.info("DEEP %d/%d %s integrated=%.1f deep=%.1f", i, len(candidates), code, fnum(r.get("integrated_score")), score)
        out = pd.DataFrame(rows).sort_values(["deep_score", "deep_coverage", "integrated_score"], ascending=False).reset_index(drop=True)
        out["deep_grade"] = out["deep_score"].map(lambda v: "S" if v >= 88 else "A" if v >= 80 else "B" if v >= 72 else "WATCH" if v >= 64 else "REJECT")
        return out

    def save_integrated(self, runtime: pd.DataFrame, stage1: pd.DataFrame, clusters: pd.DataFrame, stage2: pd.DataFrame, deep: pd.DataFrame, index_ctx: Dict[str, Any]):
        """최종 결과/섹터 이동/선정 히스토리를 빠르게 저장한다.

        Render 웹서비스에서는 큰 CSV 저장을 기본 비활성화해 최종화 단계 정지를 방지한다.
        SAVE_FULL_CSV=true 일 때만 상세 CSV를 함께 남긴다.
        """
        now = kst_now()
        stamp = now.strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
        files = {
            "runtime": self.result_dir / f"500실행유니버스_{stamp}.csv",
            "stage1": self.result_dir / f"500_1차알파RS_{stamp}.csv",
            "clusters": self.result_dir / f"군집에너지_{stamp}.csv",
            "stage2": self.result_dir / f"2차후보70_{stamp}.csv",
            "deep": self.result_dir / f"LS딥30_{stamp}.csv",
            "final": self.result_dir / f"최종상방후보_{stamp}.csv",
        }
        save_full = os.getenv("SAVE_FULL_CSV", "false").strip().lower() in {"1","true","yes","y","on"}

        if deep.empty:
            final = deep
        else:
            final = deep[
                deep["deep_grade"].isin(["S", "A", "B"]) &
                deep["stage1_state"].isin(["UP_START", "REVERSAL_ARMED", "DOWN_WEAKEN"]) &
                ((deep["cluster_state"].isin(["S2", "S3"])) | deep["asym_rs_class"].isin(["SUPER", "STRONG"]))
            ].head(self.final_top_n).copy()

        LOG.info("FINALIZE start final=%d deep=%d sectors=%d csv=%s", len(final), len(deep), len(clusters), save_full)
        if save_full:
            runtime.to_csv(files["runtime"], index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")
            stage1.to_csv(files["stage1"], index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")
            clusters.to_csv(files["clusters"], index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")
            stage2.to_csv(files["stage2"], index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")
            deep.to_csv(files["deep"], index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")
            final.to_csv(files["final"], index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")

        # 섹터 에너지: 직전 회차 대비 변화량 + 순위 이동
        sector_path = self.result_dir / f"latest_sector_energy_{self.active_venue}.json"
        prev_sector = []
        try:
            if sector_path.exists():
                prev_sector = json.loads(sector_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        except Exception:
            prev_sector = []
        prev_map = {str(x.get("ENERGY_CLUSTER")): x for x in prev_sector if isinstance(x, dict)}
        sector_rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        if not clusters.empty:
            csort = clusters.sort_values(["cluster_energy", "cluster_rotation"], ascending=False).reset_index(drop=True)
            for idx, r in csort.iterrows():
                cluster = str(r.get("ENERGY_CLUSTER", ""))
                rank = idx + 1
                energy = fnum(r.get("cluster_energy"), 0.0)
                p = prev_map.get(cluster, {})
                prev_energy = p.get("cluster_energy")
                prev_rank = p.get("rank")
                delta = None if prev_energy is None else round(energy - fnum(prev_energy), 2)
                rank_move = None if prev_rank is None else int(fnum(prev_rank, rank)) - rank
                movement = "NEW" if prev_rank is None else (f"↑{rank_move}" if rank_move > 0 else f"↓{abs(rank_move)}" if rank_move < 0 else "→")
                sector_rows.append({
                    "rank": rank,
                    "ENERGY_CLUSTER": cluster,
                    "cluster_state": str(r.get("cluster_state", "")),
                    "cluster_energy": round(energy, 2),
                    "energy_delta": delta,
                    "previous_rank": prev_rank,
                    "rank_move": rank_move,
                    "movement": movement,
                    "cluster_breadth": round(fnum(r.get("cluster_breadth"), 0.0), 4),
                    "cluster_avg_return": round(fnum(r.get("cluster_avg_return"), 0.0), 3),
                    "cluster_market_rs": round(fnum(r.get("cluster_market_rs"), 0.0), 3),
                    "cluster_turnover_accel": round(fnum(r.get("cluster_turnover_accel"), 0.0), 3),
                    "cluster_members": int(fnum(r.get("cluster_members"), 0)),
                })
        write_json_atomic(sector_path, sector_rows)

        def stock_rows(df: pd.DataFrame, selection_type: str, limit: int = 10) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
            out: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
            if df is None or df.empty:
                return out
            for _, r in df.head(limit).iterrows():
                px = fnum(r.get("stage1_close"), 0.0)
                out.append({
                    "selection_type": selection_type,
                    "symbol": str(r.get("symbol", "")).zfill(6),
                    "name": str(r.get("name", "")),
                    "current_price": px,
                    "change_pct": round(fnum(r.get("current_return_pct"), 0.0), 3),
                    "entry_price": px,
                    "MEGA_SECTOR": str(r.get("MEGA_SECTOR", "")),
                    "ENERGY_CLUSTER": str(r.get("ENERGY_CLUSTER", "")),
                    "cluster_state": str(r.get("cluster_state", "")),
                    "cluster_energy": round(fnum(r.get("cluster_energy"), 0.0), 2),
                    "market_rs": round(fnum(r.get("market_rs"), 0.0), 3),
                    "rs5": round(fnum(r.get("rs5"), 0.0), 3),
                    "rs20": round(fnum(r.get("rs20"), 0.0), 3),
                    "stage1_state": str(r.get("stage1_state", "")),
                    "integrated_score": round(fnum(r.get("integrated_score"), 0.0), 2),
                    "deep_score": round(fnum(r.get("deep_score"), 0.0), 2),
                    "deep_grade": str(r.get("deep_grade", "")),
                    "trade_strength": round(fnum(r.get("trade_strength"), 0.0), 2),
                    "foreign_net": fnum(r.get("foreign_net"), 0.0),
                    "institution_net": fnum(r.get("institution_net"), 0.0),
                    "program_net": fnum(r.get("program_net"), 0.0),
                })
            return out

        top_final = stock_rows(final, "FINAL", self.final_top_n)
        # 적격 FINAL이 없을 때도 강제매수 후보로 만들지 않고 WATCH로 명확히 분리한다.
        top_watch = stock_rows(deep, "WATCH", self.final_top_n)

        # 선정 히스토리 최대 200건. FINAL 우선, FINAL이 없으면 WATCH 상위 5개를 기록한다.
        history_path = self.result_dir / "selection_history.json"
        history: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        try:
            if history_path.exists():
                z = json.loads(history_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
                if isinstance(z, list):
                    history = z
        except Exception:
            history = []
        additions = top_final if top_final else top_watch[:5]
        stamp_kst = now.isoformat(timespec="seconds")
        prior_counts: Dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
        for h in history:
            prior_counts[str(h.get("symbol", ""))] += 1
        for row in additions:
            code = row.get("symbol", "")
            prior_counts[code] += 1
            h = dict(row)
            h["selected_at_kst"] = stamp_kst
            h["venue"] = self.active_venue
            h["session"] = str(self.session_info.get("session", ""))
            h["occurrence"] = prior_counts[code]
            history.insert(0, h)
        history = history[:env_int("SELECTION_HISTORY_MAX", 200)]
        write_json_atomic(history_path, history)

        current_index = index_ctx.get("current", {})
        summary = {
            "timestamp_kst": stamp_kst,
            "venue": self.active_venue,
            "session": str(self.session_info.get("session", "")),
            "exchgubun": self.active_exchgubun,
            "universe": int(len(runtime)),
            "clusters": int(len(clusters)),
            "stage1_valid": int((stage1["stage1_state"] != "ERROR").sum()) if not stage1.empty else 0,
            "stage2_count": int(len(stage2)),
            "deep_count": int(len(deep)),
            "final_count": int(len(final)),
            "index": {m: {k: v for k, v in z.items() if k != "error"} for m, z in current_index.items()},
            "files": {k: str(v) for k, v in files.items()} if save_full else {},
            "sector_energy": sector_rows[:30],
            "top_clusters": sector_rows[:15],
            "top_final": top_final,
            "top_watch": top_watch,
            "selection_history": history,
            "history_count": len(history),
            "universe_report": getattr(self, "last_universe_report", {}),
        }
        write_json_atomic(self.result_dir / f"latest_alpha500_{self.active_venue}.json", summary)
        write_json_atomic(self.result_dir / "latest_alpha500.json", summary)
        LOG.info("FINALIZE done final=%d watch=%d history=%d", len(top_final), len(top_watch), len(history))
        return final, summary

    def bootstrap_history(self, runtime: pd.DataFrame):
        _, _, errors = self.fast_history.get_all(runtime, bootstrap_missing=True)
        return errors

    def run_integrated(self, bootstrap_missing: bool = True, session_info: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]] = None):
        info = self.set_market_session(session_info or market_session_now())
        if not bool(info.get("scan_allowed", True)):
            raise RuntimeError(f"session not open: {info.get('session')}")
        runtime_path = str(self.result_dir / f"선정500_실행유니버스_{self.active_venue}.csv")
        runtime, ureport = self.selector.select(runtime_path, live=True)
        self.last_universe_report = dict(ureport or {})
        if self.active_venue == "NXT" and "nxt_eligible" in runtime.columns:
            runtime = runtime[runtime["nxt_eligible"].map(lambda v: v is True or str(v).strip().lower() in {"1","true","y","yes"})].copy().reset_index(drop=True)
        LOG.info("runtime universe selected=%d anchors=%d venue=%s session=%s nxt_total=%s cap_eligible=%s",
                 len(runtime), ureport.get("liquidity_anchor_in_selected", 0), self.active_venue, info.get("session"),
                 ureport.get("nxt_eligible_total"), ureport.get("cap_eligible_estimate"))
        if runtime.empty:
            raise RuntimeError(f"{self.active_venue} eligible universe empty")

        stage1, data_cache, _ = self.run_stage1_fast(runtime, bootstrap_missing=bootstrap_missing)
        stage1, index_ctx = self.attach_market_rs(stage1, data_cache)
        stage1, clusters = self.calculate_cluster_energy(stage1, data_cache)
        stage1 = self.attach_integrated_score(stage1)
        stage2 = self.select_stage2(stage1)
        deep30 = self.select_deep30(stage2)
        LOG.info("pipeline %d -> stage2=%d -> deep=%d venue=%s", len(runtime), len(stage2), len(deep30), self.active_venue)
        deep = self.run_deep_custom(deep30)
        LOG.info("pipeline deep complete=%d; finalizing venue=%s", len(deep), self.active_venue)
        final, summary = self.save_integrated(runtime, stage1, clusters, stage2, deep, index_ctx)
        LOG.info("pipeline complete final=%d venue=%s", len(final), self.active_venue)
        return runtime, stage1, clusters, stage2, deep, final, summary


def self_test(master_csv: str):
    master = pd.read_csv(master_csv, dtype=str).fillna("")
    assert len(master) == 500 and master["종목명"].nunique() == 500
    assert master["MICRO_CLUSTER"].nunique() >= 120
    assert master["ENERGY_CLUSTER"].nunique() >= 100

    # 비대칭 RS 경계 검증
    assert Alpha500Integrated._asym_class(2.1, 0.45) == "SUPER"
    assert Alpha500Integrated._asym_class(1.6, 0.60) == "STRONG"
    assert Alpha500Integrated._asym_class(1.25, 0.75) == "POSITIVE"
    assert Alpha500Integrated._asym_class(0.7, 1.2) == "WEAK"

    # t8407 overlay 검증
    hist = pd.DataFrame({
        "timestamp": pd.date_range(end=pd.Timestamp(date.today()) - pd.Timedelta(days=1), periods=80, freq="D"),
        "open": np.linspace(100, 120, 80), "high": np.linspace(102, 122, 80),
        "low": np.linspace(98, 118, 80), "close": np.linspace(101, 121, 80),
        "volume": np.linspace(1000, 2000, 80), "turnover": np.linspace(100, 200, 80),
    })
    q = {"price": 123, "open": 121, "high": 125, "low": 120, "volume": 2200, "value": 230}
    over = FastHistory500.overlay_quote(hist, q)
    assert len(over) == 81 and float(over.iloc[-1]["close"]) == 123
    print("SELF_TEST PASS")
    print(f"master=500 micro={master['MICRO_CLUSTER'].nunique()} energy={master['ENERGY_CLUSTER'].nunique()}")


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--master", default=os.getenv("UNIVERSE_MASTER", "섹터마스터_500종목_V3.csv"))
    ap.add_argument("--self-test", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--no-bootstrap", action="store_true", help="history cache 없는 종목을 API로 bootstrap하지 않음")
    ap.add_argument("--bootstrap-only", action="store_true")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    if args.self_test:
        self_test(args.master)
        return
    engine = Alpha500Integrated(args.master)
    if args.bootstrap_only:
        runtime, report = engine.selector.select(str(engine.result_dir / "선정500_실행유니버스.csv"), live=True)
        errors = engine.bootstrap_history(runtime)
        print(json.dumps({"universe": report, "history_errors": errors[:50], "error_count": len(errors)}, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
        return
    *_, final, summary = engine.run_integrated(bootstrap_missing=not args.no_bootstrap)
    print(json.dumps(summary, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default=str))
    if not final.empty:
        print(final[[c for c in ["symbol","name","ENERGY_CLUSTER","cluster_state","cluster_energy","market_rs","rs5","asym_rs_class","stage1_state","deep_score","deep_grade"] if c in final.columns]].to_string(index=False))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
오백종목_유동성군집_선정엔진_V1_0.py

목적
----
1) 사용자가 합의한 500종목 세부군집 마스터를 기본 Universe로 사용한다.
2) LS KRX 거래대금 상위(t1463)를 매 거래일 저장해 고유동성 상위 100을 강제 포함한다.
3) LS 시가총액 상위(t1444)를 일별 캐시해 시총 1,000억원 미만을 가능한 범위에서 제거한다.
4) 신규 고유동성 종목이 마스터 밖에서 등장하면 500개 안으로 편입하고, 과대 군집의 낮은 우선순위 종목을 교체한다.
5) 동적 편입 종목은 군집이 검증되기 전에는 ENERGY_EXCLUDE=Y로 두어 섹터에너지를 왜곡하지 않는다.

데이터 원칙
-----------
- KRX 전용. NXT/통합 시세를 섞지 않는다.
- ETF/ETN/우선주/SPAC/REIT는 제외한다.
- 주문 API를 사용하지 않는다.
- LS_APP_KEY / LS_APP_SECRET만 사용하며 계좌번호/계좌비밀번호는 사용하지 않는다.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import json
import math
import os
import re
import sqlite3
import time
from collections import Counter, defaultdict
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import date, datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, Iterable, List, Mapping, Optional, Sequence, Tuple

import pandas as pd

from 전체시장_알파하이킨아시_KIS_LS_스캐너_V1_0 import (
    BrokerError,

    SymbolInfo,

def env_int(name: str, default: int) -> int:
    try:
        return int(os.getenv(name, str(default)))
    except Exception:
        return default


def env_float(name: str, default: float) -> float:
    try:
        return float(os.getenv(name, str(default)))
    except Exception:
        return default


def fnum(v: Any, default: float = 0.0) -> float:
    try:
        if v in (None, "", " "):
            return default
        x = float(str(v).replace(",", ""))
        return x if math.isfinite(x) else default
    except Exception:
        return default


def norm_name(v: str) -> str:
    """종목명 매칭용: 공백/일부 기호/대소문자 차이를 제거한다."""
    s = str(v or "").strip().upper()
    s = s.replace("㈜", "").replace("주식회사", "")
    return re.sub(r"[^0-9A-Z가-힣]", "", s)


NAME_ALIASES = {
    # LS 마스터/시장 관행의 표기 차이를 보정하기 위한 최소 별칭.
    norm_name("JYP Ent."): [norm_name("JYP Ent."), norm_name("JYP엔터테인먼트")],
    norm_name("CJ ENM"): [norm_name("CJ ENM"), norm_name("CJENM")],
    norm_name("지앤비에스 에코"): [norm_name("지앤비에스 에코"), norm_name("지앤비에스에코")],
    norm_name("HD현대일렉트릭"): [norm_name("HD현대일렉트릭")],
    norm_name("HD현대마린엔진"): [norm_name("HD현대마린엔진")],
}


class AdaptiveLSProvider(LSProvider):
    """TR별 공식 유량에 맞춰 최소 호출 간격을 분리한 LS read-only provider."""

    TR_INTERVAL = {
        "t8407": 0.22,  # 5/sec
        "t9945": 0.52,  # 2/sec
        "t1444": 0.52,  # 2/sec
        "t1463": 1.05,  # 1/sec
        "t1511": 0.12,  # 10/sec
        "t8429": 1.05,  # 1/sec
        "t1305": 1.05,  # 1/sec
        "t1475": 1.05,
        "t1702": 1.05,
        "t1637": 1.05,
        "t8450": 0.11,  # 10/sec
        "t8451": 1.05,  # 1/sec
        "t8454": 1.05,  # 1/sec
        "t1102": 0.11,  # 10/sec
    }

    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self._tr_last: Dict[str, float] = {}

    def _wait_tr(self, tr_cd: str):
        gap = self.TR_INTERVAL.get(tr_cd, 1.05)
        last = self._tr_last.get(tr_cd, 0.0)
        wait = gap - (time.monotonic() - last)
        if wait > 0:
            time.sleep(wait)
        self._tr_last[tr_cd] = time.monotonic()

    def _post_tr(self, path: str, tr_cd: str, body: dict) -> requests.Response:
        self._wait_tr(tr_cd)
        r = self.s.post(
            self.base + path,
            headers=self._headers(tr_cd),
            json=body,
            timeout=self.timeout,
        )
        if r.status_code != 200:
            raise BrokerError(f"LS {tr_cd} HTTP {r.status_code}: {r.text[:240]}")
        return r


@dataclass
class LiquidityRow:
    code: str
    name: str
    market: str
    trade_value: float
    market_cap_100m: Optional[float]
    rank: int
    return_pct: float = 0.0


class LiquidityHistory:
    def __init__(self, path: Path):
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.conn = sqlite3.connect(path)
        self.conn.execute(
            """
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS liquidity (
              trade_date TEXT NOT NULL,
              code TEXT NOT NULL,
              name TEXT,
              market TEXT,
              trade_value REAL,
              market_cap_100m REAL,
              rank_no INTEGER,
              return_pct REAL,
              PRIMARY KEY(trade_date, code)
            )
            """
        )
        self.conn.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_liq_date ON liquidity(trade_date)")
        self.conn.commit()

    def upsert(self, trade_date: str, rows: Sequence[LiquidityRow]):
        if not rows:
            return
        self.conn.executemany(
            """
            INSERT INTO liquidity(trade_date,code,name,market,trade_value,market_cap_100m,rank_no,return_pct)
            VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)
            ON CONFLICT(trade_date,code) DO UPDATE SET
              name=excluded.name, market=excluded.market, trade_value=excluded.trade_value,
              market_cap_100m=excluded.market_cap_100m, rank_no=excluded.rank_no,
              return_pct=excluded.return_pct
            """,
            [
                (trade_date, r.code, r.name, r.market, r.trade_value, r.market_cap_100m, r.rank, r.return_pct)
                for r in rows
            ],
        )
        self.conn.commit()

    def rolling(self, days: int = 20) -> pd.DataFrame:
        dates = pd.read_sql_query(
            "SELECT DISTINCT trade_date FROM liquidity ORDER BY trade_date DESC LIMIT ?",
            self.conn,
            params=(days,),
        )
        if dates.empty:
            return pd.DataFrame()
        use = dates["trade_date"].astype(str).tolist()
        q = f"""
        SELECT code,
               MAX(name) name,
               MAX(market) market,
               AVG(trade_value) avg_trade_value,
               MAX(market_cap_100m) market_cap_100m,
               AVG(rank_no) avg_rank,
               COUNT(DISTINCT trade_date) coverage_days,
               AVG(return_pct) avg_return_pct
        FROM liquidity
        WHERE trade_date IN ({','.join(['?'] * len(use))})
        GROUP BY code
        """
        df = pd.read_sql_query(q, self.conn, params=use)
        if df.empty:
            return df
        max_days = max(1, len(use))
        df["coverage_ratio"] = df["coverage_days"] / max_days
        # 새 종목이 며칠만 등장해 평균이 과대평가되는 것을 줄인다.
        df["liquidity_score"] = df["avg_trade_value"] * (0.65 + 0.35 * df["coverage_ratio"])
        return df.sort_values(["liquidity_score", "avg_rank"], ascending=[False, True]).reset_index(drop=True)


class Universe500Selector:
    def __init__(self, master_csv: str, ls: Optional[AdaptiveLSProvider] = None):
        self.master_csv = Path(master_csv)
        self.target = env_int("UNIVERSE_TARGET", 500)
        self.anchor_n = env_int("LIQUIDITY_ANCHORS", 100)
        self.liq_window = env_int("LIQUIDITY_WINDOW_DAYS", 20)
        self.min_cap_100m = env_float("PULSE_MIN_MARKET_CAP_100M", 1000.0)
        self.scan_rank_n = env_int("LIQUIDITY_SCAN_N", 220)
        self.min_cluster_keep = env_int("ENERGY_MIN_MEMBERS", 3)
        self.data_dir = Path(os.getenv("ALPHA500_DATA_DIR", "/data"))
        try:
            self.data_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        except Exception:
            self.data_dir = Path("./data")
            self.data_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.result_dir = Path(os.getenv("RESULT_DIR", "./results"))
        self.result_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.history = LiquidityHistory(self.data_dir / "alpha500_liquidity.sqlite3")
        self.ls = ls or AdaptiveLSProvider()
        self._universe: Optional[List[SymbolInfo]] = None
        self._cap_cache: Optional[Dict[str, float]] = None
        self.exchgubun = "K"

    def set_exchange(self, exchgubun: str):
        x = str(exchgubun or "K").upper()
        if x not in {"K", "N"}:
            raise ValueError(f"unsupported LS exchgubun={x}")
        self.exchgubun = x
        # 유동성 랭킹 히스토리도 거래소별 DB로 분리한다.
        self.history = LiquidityHistory(self.data_dir / f"alpha500_liquidity_{x}.sqlite3")

    def load_master(self) -> pd.DataFrame:
        df = pd.read_csv(self.master_csv, dtype=str).fillna("")
        required = ["종목명", "MEGA_SECTOR", "SECTOR", "MICRO_CLUSTER", "ENERGY_CLUSTER", "역할"]
        missing = [c for c in required if c not in df.columns]
        if missing:
            raise ValueError(f"500 마스터 컬럼 누락: {missing}")
        if len(df) != self.target:
            raise ValueError(f"마스터 행수 {len(df)} != target {self.target}")
        if df["종목명"].duplicated().any():
            dup = df[df["종목명"].duplicated(keep=False)]["종목명"].tolist()
            raise ValueError(f"마스터 종목 중복: {dup[:10]}")
        return df

    def get_ls_universe(self) -> List[SymbolInfo]:
        if self._universe is None:
            self._universe = [x for x in self.ls.get_universe() if is_probably_common_stock(x)]
        return self._universe

    def _name_index(self, universe: Sequence[SymbolInfo]) -> Tuple[Dict[str, SymbolInfo], Dict[str, SymbolInfo]]:
        by_name: Dict[str, SymbolInfo] = {}
        by_code: Dict[str, SymbolInfo] = {}
        for x in universe:
            by_code[x.code] = x
            by_name.setdefault(norm_name(x.name), x)
        return by_name, by_code

    def resolve_master(self, master: pd.DataFrame, universe: Sequence[SymbolInfo]) -> Tuple[pd.DataFrame, List[str]]:
        by_name, _ = self._name_index(universe)
        rows = []
        unresolved = []
        for _, r in master.iterrows():
            name = str(r["종목명"]).strip()
            keys = NAME_ALIASES.get(norm_name(name), [norm_name(name)])
            item = next((by_name[k] for k in keys if k in by_name), None)
            if not item:
                unresolved.append(name)
                continue
            d = dict(r)
            d.update({"code": item.code, "name": item.name, "market": item.market, "ENERGY_EXCLUDE": "N", "nxt_eligible": bool(getattr(item, "nxt", False))})
            rows.append(d)
        return pd.DataFrame(rows), unresolved

    def _rank_pages(self, gubun: str, jnilgubun: str, max_rows: int) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        idx = 0
        seen = set()
        for _ in range(20):
            body = {
                "t1463InBlock": {
                    "gubun": str(gubun),
                    "jnilgubun": str(jnilgubun),
                    "jc_num": 0,
                    "sprice": 0,
                    "eprice": 0,
                    "volume": 0,
                    "idx": idx,
                    "jc_num2": 0,
                    "exchgubun": self.exchgubun,
                }
            }
            try:
                j = self.ls._post_tr("/stock/high-item", "t1463", body).json()
            except Exception as e:
                # LS 유량제어(IGW00201)나 일시 오류가 유동성 랭킹 하나 때문에
                # 전체 500종목 스캔/WS 시작을 막지 않도록 soft-fail 한다.
                LOG.warning("t1463 rank unavailable; continue without fresh rank: %s", str(e)[:220])
                break
            page = j.get("t1463OutBlock1") or []
            rows.extend(page)
            nxt = int(fnum((j.get("t1463OutBlock") or {}).get("idx"), 0))
            if not page or len(rows) >= max_rows or nxt <= 0 or nxt == idx or nxt in seen:
                break
            seen.add(nxt)
            idx = nxt
        return rows[:max_rows]

    def fetch_liquidity_snapshot(self, universe: Sequence[SymbolInfo]) -> List[LiquidityRow]:
        _, by_code = self._name_index(universe)
        raw = self._rank_pages("0", "1", self.scan_rank_n)
        if not raw or sum(fnum(x.get("value"), 0) for x in raw) <= 0:
            raw = self._rank_pages("0", "2", self.scan_rank_n)
        out: List[LiquidityRow] = []
        for rank, z in enumerate(raw, 1):
            code = str(z.get("shcode", "")).strip().zfill(6)
            item = by_code.get(code)
            if not item or not is_probably_common_stock(item):
                continue
            cap = fnum(z.get("total"), float("nan"))
            cap_v = None if not math.isfinite(cap) or cap <= 0 else cap
            if cap_v is not None and cap_v < self.min_cap_100m:
                continue
            out.append(
                LiquidityRow(
                    code=code,
                    name=item.name,
                    market=item.market,
                    trade_value=fnum(z.get("value"), 0.0),
                    market_cap_100m=cap_v,
                    rank=rank,
                    return_pct=fnum(z.get("diff"), 0.0),
                )
            )
        return out

    def _fetch_cap_market(self, upcode: str, max_pages: int = 30) -> Dict[str, float]:
        out: Dict[str, float] = {}
        idx = 0
        seen = set()
        for _ in range(max_pages):
            body = {"t1444InBlock": {"upcode": upcode, "idx": idx}}
            j = self.ls._post_tr("/stock/high-item", "t1444", body).json()
            page = j.get("t1444OutBlock1") or []
            for z in page:
                code = str(z.get("shcode", "")).strip().zfill(6)
                cap = fnum(z.get("total"), 0.0)
                if re.fullmatch(r"\d{6}", code) and cap > 0:
                    out[code] = cap
            nxt = int(fnum((j.get("t1444OutBlock") or {}).get("idx"), 0))
            # 순위형이므로 페이지의 최저 시총이 1000억 아래로 내려가면 이후도 필요 없음.
            page_caps = [fnum(x.get("total"), 0.0) for x in page if fnum(x.get("total"), 0.0) > 0]
            if page_caps and min(page_caps) < self.min_cap_100m:
                break
            if not page or nxt <= 0 or nxt == idx or nxt in seen:
                break
            seen.add(nxt)
            idx = nxt
        return out

    def get_cap_map(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
        if self._cap_cache is not None:
            return self._cap_cache
        today = date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d")
        p = self.data_dir / f"cap_map_{today}.json"
        if p.exists():
            try:
                self._cap_cache = {k: float(v) for k, v in json.loads(p.read_text(encoding="utf-8")).items()}
                return self._cap_cache
            except Exception:
                pass
        cap = {}
        for upcode in ("001", "301"):
            cap.update(self._fetch_cap_market(upcode))
        p.write_text(json.dumps(cap, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")
        self._cap_cache = cap
        return cap

    def update_liquidity_history(self, universe: Sequence[SymbolInfo]) -> pd.DataFrame:
        snap = self.fetch_liquidity_snapshot(universe)
        # 시장일자는 LS snapshot에 별도 필드가 없으므로 실행일 기준 저장. 휴장일 반복은 PK로 덮어쓴다.
        self.history.upsert(date.today().isoformat(), snap)
        return self.history.rolling(self.liq_window)

    @staticmethod
    def _role_priority(role: str) -> int:
        return {
            "LEADER_SENSOR": 100,
            "CORE": 80,
            "LIQUIDITY_ANCHOR": 90,
            "RELATED_SENSOR": 70,
            "FOLLOWER": 50,
            "EARLY": 40,
            "THEME_WATCH": 95,
        }.get(str(role), 50)

    def _remove_candidates(self, df: pd.DataFrame, protected_codes: set[str]) -> List[int]:
        counts = Counter(df["ENERGY_CLUSTER"].astype(str))
        scored = []
        for idx, r in df.iterrows():
            code = str(r.get("code", ""))
            if code in protected_codes:
                continue
            role = str(r.get("역할", ""))
            if role in {"LEADER_SENSOR", "THEME_WATCH"}:
                continue
            cluster = str(r.get("ENERGY_CLUSTER", ""))
            ccount = counts.get(cluster, 0)
            if cluster and cluster not in {"UNMAPPED_LIQUIDITY", "미분류"} and ccount <= self.min_cluster_keep:
                continue
            # 낮은 역할 우선 + 큰 군집에서 우선 제거.
            scored.append((self._role_priority(role), -ccount, idx))
        scored.sort()
        return [idx for _, _, idx in scored]

    def select(self, output_csv: Optional[str] = None, live: bool = True) -> Tuple[pd.DataFrame, Dict[str, Any]]:
        master = self.load_master()
        universe_all = self.get_ls_universe() if live else []
        if not live:
            raise RuntimeError("select(live=False)는 self_test 전용 merge 함수 대신 사용하지 마세요")

        # NXT에서는 'KRX용 500개를 만든 뒤 NXT 종목만 필터'하지 않는다.
        # LS t9945의 nxt_chk=True 종목만으로 처음부터 별도 유니버스를 구성한다.
        # 기존 294개 현상은 generic 500과 NXT 대상종목의 교집합만 남긴 구조 +
        # 동적 보충행의 nxt_eligible 누락이 겹친 것이 원인이었다.
        nxt_total = sum(1 for x in universe_all if bool(getattr(x, "nxt", False)))
        if self.exchgubun == "N":
            universe = [x for x in universe_all if bool(getattr(x, "nxt", False))]
        else:
            universe = list(universe_all)

        resolved, unresolved = self.resolve_master(master, universe)
        cap_map = self.get_cap_map()
        cap_eligible_count = sum(1 for x in universe if cap_map.get(x.code) is None or fnum(cap_map.get(x.code), 0) >= self.min_cap_100m) if cap_map else len(universe)
        target_n = min(self.target, cap_eligible_count) if self.exchgubun == "N" else self.target
        if cap_map:
            resolved["market_cap_100m"] = resolved["code"].map(cap_map)
            # 실제 cap map에 들어온 종목이 기준 미달이면 제거. cap map 미수록은 아직 검증대기.
            resolved = resolved[
                resolved["market_cap_100m"].isna() | (resolved["market_cap_100m"] >= self.min_cap_100m)
            ].copy()
        else:
            resolved["market_cap_100m"] = pd.NA

        rolling = self.update_liquidity_history(universe)
        anchors = rolling.head(self.anchor_n).copy() if not rolling.empty else pd.DataFrame()
        anchor_codes = set(anchors["code"].astype(str)) if not anchors.empty else set()
        anchor_meta = anchors.set_index("code").to_dict("index") if not anchors.empty else {}

        # static row에 anchor 표기
        resolved["LIQUIDITY_ANCHOR"] = resolved["code"].map(lambda x: "Y" if x in anchor_codes else "N")
        resolved["liquidity_score"] = resolved["code"].map(lambda x: fnum(anchor_meta.get(x, {}).get("liquidity_score"), 0.0))
        resolved["liquidity_rank"] = resolved["code"].map(lambda x: fnum(anchor_meta.get(x, {}).get("avg_rank"), 9999.0))

        by_code = {x.code: x for x in universe}
        current_codes = set(resolved["code"].astype(str))
        extra_rows = []
        for _, a in anchors.iterrows():
            code = str(a["code"])
            if code in current_codes:
                continue
            item = by_code.get(code)
            if not item:
                continue
            extra_rows.append({
                "종목명": item.name,
                "BUCKET_BASE": "LIQUIDITY_ANCHOR",
                "MEGA_SECTOR": "미분류",
                "SECTOR": "미분류_유동성",
                "MICRO_CLUSTER": "미분류_유동성",
                "ENERGY_CLUSTER": "UNMAPPED_LIQUIDITY",
                "ENERGY_LEVEL": "EXCLUDE_UNTIL_MAPPED",
                "SECONDARY_TAG": "",
                "역할": "LIQUIDITY_ANCHOR",
                "에너지기본가중": "0.00",
                "매수후보": "Y",
                "시총1000억필터": "PASS_BY_T1463" if fnum(a.get("market_cap_100m"), 0) >= self.min_cap_100m else "검증대기",
                "활성상태": "ACTIVE",
                "테마세부": "",
                "운용메모": "동적 고유동성 강제편입; 군집 매핑 전 섹터에너지 제외",
                "출처URL": "LS:t1463",
                "code": code,
                "name": item.name,
                "market": item.market,
                "ENERGY_EXCLUDE": "Y",
                "market_cap_100m": a.get("market_cap_100m"),
                "LIQUIDITY_ANCHOR": "Y",
                "liquidity_score": a.get("liquidity_score", 0),
                "liquidity_rank": a.get("avg_rank", 9999),
                "nxt_eligible": bool(getattr(item, "nxt", False)),
            })
            current_codes.add(code)
        if extra_rows:
            resolved = pd.concat([resolved, pd.DataFrame(extra_rows)], ignore_index=True, sort=False)

        # 500 초과분 제거: anchor/이벤트/leader 보호, 군집 최소 구성 유지.
        if len(resolved) > target_n:
            protected = anchor_codes | set(
                resolved.loc[resolved["역할"].isin(["LEADER_SENSOR", "THEME_WATCH"]), "code"].astype(str)
            )
            remove_idx = self._remove_candidates(resolved, protected)
            drop_n = len(resolved) - target_n
            resolved = resolved.drop(remove_idx[:drop_n])

        # 500 미만이면 고유동성 순 → 나머지 LS 보통주 순으로 채운다.
        if len(resolved) < target_n:
            existing = set(resolved["code"].astype(str))
            fill_codes = []
            if not rolling.empty:
                fill_codes.extend([str(x) for x in rolling["code"] if str(x) not in existing])
            fill_codes.extend([x.code for x in universe if x.code not in existing and x.code not in fill_codes])
            fills = []
            for code in fill_codes:
                if len(resolved) + len(fills) >= target_n:
                    break
                item = by_code.get(code)
                if not item or not is_probably_common_stock(item):
                    continue
                cap = cap_map.get(code)
                if cap is not None and cap < self.min_cap_100m:
                    continue
                fills.append({
                    "종목명": item.name, "BUCKET_BASE": "ROTATION_FILL", "MEGA_SECTOR": "미분류",
                    "SECTOR": "미분류_보충", "MICRO_CLUSTER": "미분류_보충",
                    "ENERGY_CLUSTER": "UNMAPPED_LIQUIDITY", "ENERGY_LEVEL": "EXCLUDE_UNTIL_MAPPED",
                    "SECONDARY_TAG": "", "역할": "FOLLOWER", "에너지기본가중": "0.00",
                    "매수후보": "Y", "시총1000억필터": "PASS" if cap else "검증대기",
                    "활성상태": "ACTIVE", "테마세부": "", "운용메모": "500 유지용 동적 보충",
                    "출처URL": "LS:t9945/t1444", "code": code, "name": item.name, "market": item.market,
                    "ENERGY_EXCLUDE": "Y", "market_cap_100m": cap, "LIQUIDITY_ANCHOR": "N",
                    "liquidity_score": 0.0, "liquidity_rank": 9999,
                    "nxt_eligible": bool(getattr(item, "nxt", False)),
                })
                existing.add(code)
            if fills:
                resolved = pd.concat([resolved, pd.DataFrame(fills)], ignore_index=True, sort=False)

        # 마지막 안전장치
        resolved = resolved.drop_duplicates("code", keep="first").copy()
        if len(resolved) != target_n:
            raise RuntimeError(f"실행 Universe를 목표 {target_n}개로 구성하지 못함: {len(resolved)} (venue={self.exchgubun}, source={len(universe)}, cap_eligible={cap_eligible_count}, unresolved={len(unresolved)})")

        # 기존 scanner가 읽을 수 있는 컬럼을 앞에 둔다.
        resolved["code"] = resolved["code"].astype(str).str.zfill(6)
        resolved["name"] = resolved["name"].astype(str)
        resolved["market"] = resolved["market"].astype(str)
        resolved["runtime_rank"] = range(1, len(resolved) + 1)
        cols_first = ["code", "name", "market", "runtime_rank", "LIQUIDITY_ANCHOR", "liquidity_score", "liquidity_rank", "market_cap_100m", "ENERGY_EXCLUDE"]
        cols = cols_first + [c for c in resolved.columns if c not in cols_first]
        resolved = resolved[cols]

        if output_csv is None:
            output_csv = str(self.result_dir / "선정500_실행유니버스.csv")
        Path(output_csv).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        resolved.to_csv(output_csv, index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")

        report = {
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
            "target": self.target,
            "effective_target": target_n,
            "selected": len(resolved),
            "source_universe_total": len(universe_all),
            "nxt_eligible_total": nxt_total,
            "venue_source_total": len(universe),
            "cap_eligible_estimate": cap_eligible_count,
            "exchgubun": self.exchgubun,
            "liquidity_anchor_target": self.anchor_n,
            "liquidity_anchor_in_selected": int((resolved["LIQUIDITY_ANCHOR"] == "Y").sum()),
            "unresolved_static_names": unresolved,
            "energy_excluded_unmapped": int((resolved["ENERGY_EXCLUDE"] == "Y").sum()),
            "output_csv": output_csv,
        }
        (self.result_dir / "latest_universe500.json").write_text(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
        return resolved, report


def self_test(master_csv: str):
    df = pd.read_csv(master_csv, dtype=str).fillna("")
    assert len(df) == 500, len(df)
    assert df["종목명"].nunique() == 500
    assert df["MICRO_CLUSTER"].nunique() >= 120
    assert df["ENERGY_CLUSTER"].nunique() >= 100
    # 군집 fallback 설계가 실제로 존재하는지 확인
    counts = df.groupby("ENERGY_CLUSTER")["종목명"].count()
    assert (counts >= 3).mean() > 0.60
    print("SELF_TEST PASS")
    print(f"rows={len(df)} unique={df['종목명'].nunique()} micro={df['MICRO_CLUSTER'].nunique()} energy={df['ENERGY_CLUSTER'].nunique()}")


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--master", default=os.getenv("UNIVERSE_MASTER", "섹터마스터_500종목_V3.csv"))
    ap.add_argument("--output", default=os.getenv("RUNTIME_UNIVERSE", "./results/선정500_실행유니버스.csv"))
    ap.add_argument("--self-test", action="store_true")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    if args.self_test:
        self_test(args.master)
        return
    s = Universe500Selector(args.master)
    df, report = s.select(args.output, live=True)
    print(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
    print(df[["code", "name", "market", "LIQUIDITY_ANCHOR", "ENERGY_CLUSTER", "역할"]].head(30).to_string(index=False))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

KST = timezone(timedelta(hours=9))

def now_kst() -> datetime:
    return datetime.now(KST)

def now_kst_iso() -> str:
    return now_kst().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
