def set_progress(phase: str, current: int = 0, total: int = 0, message: str = ""):
    with LOCK:
        STATE["phase"] = phase
        STATE["progress_current"] = int(current or 0)
        STATE["progress_total"] = int(total or 0)
        STATE["progress_message"] = message or phase


class EngineProgressHandler(logging.Handler):
    """통합엔진 로그를 화면 진행률로 변환한다."""
    def emit(self, record):
        try:
            msg = record.getMessage()
            m = re.search(r"history bootstrap/refresh required=(\d+)", msg)
            if m:
                n = int(m.group(1)); set_progress("HISTORY", 0, n, f"과거봉 구축 0/{n}"); return
            m = re.search(r"history bootstrap (\d+)/(\d+)", msg)
            if m:
                a,b = map(int,m.groups()); set_progress("HISTORY", a, b, f"과거봉 구축 {a}/{b}"); return
            m = re.search(r"FAST STAGE1 (\d+)/(\d+)", msg)
            if m:
                a,b = map(int,m.groups()); set_progress("STAGE1", a, b, f"Alpha Heikin-Ashi 1차 {a}/{b}"); return
            m = re.search(r"pipeline (\d+) -> stage2=(\d+) -> deep=(\d+)", msg)
            if m:
                uni,s2,dp = map(int,m.groups()); set_progress("STAGE2", s2, max(s2,1), f"{STATE.get('venue','-')} {uni}종목 · Stage2 {s2}개 · Deep {dp}개"); return
            m = re.search(r"DEEP (\d+)/(\d+)", msg)
            if m:
                a,b = map(int,m.groups()); set_progress("DEEP", a, b, f"LS 딥스캔 {a}/{b}"); return
            if "finalizing" in msg.lower() or "FINALIZE start" in msg:
                set_progress("FINALIZING", 0, 1, "최종 후보·섹터 이동·히스토리 저장 중"); return
            if "FINALIZE done" in msg or "pipeline complete" in msg:
                set_progress("COMPLETE", 1, 1, "판정 완료"); return
        except Exception:
            pass


_progress_handler = EngineProgressHandler()
_progress_handler.setLevel(logging.INFO)
logging.getLogger("alpha500_integrated").addHandler(_progress_handler)


def load_latest():
    p = RESULT_DIR / "latest_alpha500.json"
    if not p.exists():
        return None
    try:
        return json.loads(p.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except Exception:
        return None


def get_engine():
    global ENGINE
    if ENGINE is None:
        ENGINE = Alpha500Integrated(MASTER)
    return ENGINE

def _quote_pct(q):
    try:
        v = q.get("diff")
        return float(v) if v not in (None, "") else None
    except Exception:
        return None

def _quote_price(q):
    try:
        v = float(q.get("price") or 0)
        return v if v > 0 else None
    except Exception:
        return None

def _live_targets(summary):
    """화면에서 실시간으로 추적할 종목과 시장 구분을 반환한다."""
    if not isinstance(summary, dict):
        return {}
    targets = {}
    for key in ("top_final", "top_watch"):
        for r in summary.get(key) or []:
            c = str(r.get("symbol", "")).zfill(6)
            if c.isdigit() and len(c) == 6:
                targets.setdefault(c, str(r.get("market") or "").upper())
    for r in (summary.get("selection_history") or [])[:20]:
        c = str(r.get("symbol", "")).zfill(6)
        if c.isdigit() and len(c) == 6:
            targets.setdefault(c, str(r.get("market") or "").upper())
    limit = max(1, int(os.getenv("LIVE_QUOTE_MAX", "30")))
    return dict(list(targets.items())[:limit])


def _live_codes(summary):
    return list(_live_targets(summary).keys())


def _quote_is_fresh(code: str, max_age: int = WS_STALE_SECONDS) -> bool:
    with LIVE_LOCK:
        meta = dict(LIVE_QUOTE_META.get(code) or {})
    t = float(meta.get("recv_monotonic") or 0.0)
    return bool(t and (time.monotonic() - t) <= max_age)


def _quote_source(code: str) -> str:
    with LIVE_LOCK:
        return str((LIVE_QUOTE_META.get(code) or {}).get("source") or "")


def _set_live_quote(code: str, q: dict, source: str, venue: str = ""):
    global LIVE_UPDATED_KST
    if not code or not isinstance(q, dict):
        return
    q2 = dict(q)
    q2["symbol"] = code
    q2["_source"] = source
    q2["_updated_kst"] = now_kst_iso()
    with LIVE_LOCK:
        LIVE_QUOTES[code] = q2
        LIVE_QUOTE_META[code] = {
            "recv_monotonic": time.monotonic(),
            "updated_kst": q2["_updated_kst"],
            "source": source,
            "venue": str(venue or "").upper(),
        }
        LIVE_UPDATED_KST = q2["_updated_kst"]


def _flatten_dict(obj):
    out = {}
    if isinstance(obj, dict):
        for k, v in obj.items():
            if isinstance(v, dict):
                out.update(_flatten_dict(v))
            elif not isinstance(v, (list, tuple)):
                out[str(k)] = v
    return out


def _first_value(d, keys, default=None):
    for k in keys:
        v = d.get(k)
        if v not in (None, ""):
            return v
    return default


def _extract_ws_quote(payload):
    """S3_/K3_/NS3 실시간 체결 메시지를 공통 quote 형식으로 정규화한다."""
    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
        return None, None, None
    header = payload.get("header") if isinstance(payload.get("header"), dict) else {}
    body = payload.get("body") if isinstance(payload.get("body"), dict) else payload
    flat = _flatten_dict(body)
    tr_cd = str(header.get("tr_cd") or flat.get("tr_cd") or "").strip()
    code = str(_first_value(flat, ["shcode", "expcode", "symbol", "code", "isu_no"], "")).strip()
    if code.startswith("A") and len(code) == 7:
        code = code[1:]
    if not (code.isdigit() and len(code) == 6):
        tr_key = str(header.get("tr_key") or "").strip()
        if tr_key.isdigit() and len(tr_key) == 6:
            code = tr_key
    if not (code.isdigit() and len(code) == 6):
        return None, None, tr_cd
    price = _first_value(flat, ["price", "cheprice", "curpr", "close", "last"])
    diff = _first_value(flat, ["drate", "diff", "change_rate", "rate", "chg_rate"])
    change = _first_value(flat, ["change", "chg", "change_price"])
    volume = _first_value(flat, ["volume", "cvolume", "accvolume", "cum_volume"])
    value = _first_value(flat, ["value", "accvalue", "cum_value"])
    q = {"price": price, "diff": diff, "change": change, "volume": volume, "value": value}
    if _quote_price(q) is None:
        return None, None, tr_cd
    return code, q, tr_cd


def _ws_target_map(summary, venue):
    """판정 완료를 기다리지 않고 WS를 먼저 살리는 대상 집합을 만든다.

    1순위: 직전/현재 판정의 후보+히스토리. 세션이 바뀌어도 종목코드는 재사용하되
           현재 venue의 실시간 채널로 새로 구독한다.
    2순위: 결과 파일이 없거나 비어 있으면 LS 종목마스터에서 소수 probe 종목을 사용한다.
    """
    targets = _live_targets(summary) if isinstance(summary, dict) else {}
    if targets:
        return targets
    try:
        engine = get_engine()
        universe = engine.selector.get_ls_universe()
        if venue == "NXT":
            universe = [x for x in universe if bool(getattr(x, "nxt", False))]
        limit = max(5, min(30, int(os.getenv("WS_BOOTSTRAP_MAX", "20"))))
        return {x.code: str(x.market or "").upper() for x in universe[:limit]}
    except Exception as e:
        logging.getLogger("alpha500_ws").warning("WS bootstrap targets unavailable: %s", str(e)[:220])
        return {}


def _ws_subscriptions(summary, venue):
    targets = _ws_target_map(summary, venue)
    subs = []
    for code, market in targets.items():
        if venue == "NXT":
            subs.append(("NS3", code))
        else:
            m = str(market or "").upper()
            if "KOSDAQ" in m or m in {"Q", "2"}:
                subs.append(("K3_", code))
            elif "KOSPI" in m or m in {"P", "1"}:
                subs.append(("S3_", code))
            else:
                # market 표기가 없으면 LS master에서 시장구분을 보강한다.
                try:
                    item = next((x for x in get_engine().selector.get_ls_universe() if x.code == code), None)
                    mm = str(getattr(item, "market", "") or "").upper()
                    if "KOSDAQ" in mm:
                        subs.append(("K3_", code))
                    elif "KOSPI" in mm:
                        subs.append(("S3_", code))
                except Exception:
                    continue
    return subs


def _set_ws_state(status, subscribed=None, channels=None, error=None, last_message=False, rest_fallback=None, tr_cd=None, symbol=None, connected=False, tick=False):
    changed = False
    snapshot = None
    with LOCK:
        prev = (STATE.get("ws_status"), STATE.get("ws_subscribed"), tuple(STATE.get("ws_channels") or []), STATE.get("ws_error"))
        STATE["ws_status"] = status
        if subscribed is not None:
            STATE["ws_subscribed"] = int(subscribed)
        if channels is not None:
            STATE["ws_channels"] = list(channels)
        STATE["ws_error"] = error
        if connected:
            STATE["ws_connected_kst"] = now_kst_iso()
        if last_message:
            STATE["ws_last_message_kst"] = now_kst_iso()
        if tr_cd:
            STATE["ws_last_tr_cd"] = str(tr_cd)
        if symbol:
            STATE["ws_last_symbol"] = str(symbol)
        if tick:
            STATE["ws_tick_count"] = int(STATE.get("ws_tick_count") or 0) + 1
        if rest_fallback is not None:
            STATE["rest_fallback_active"] = bool(rest_fallback)
        cur = (STATE.get("ws_status"), STATE.get("ws_subscribed"), tuple(STATE.get("ws_channels") or []), STATE.get("ws_error"))
        changed = cur != prev
        snapshot = cur
    if changed:
        logging.getLogger("alpha500_ws").info(
            "WS STATE status=%s subscribed=%s channels=%s error=%s",
            snapshot[0], snapshot[1], list(snapshot[2]), snapshot[3] or ""
        )


def live_ws_loop():
    """LS WebSocket 1순위 실시간 시세. 세션/후보 변경 시 안전하게 재구독한다."""
    log = logging.getLogger("alpha500_ws")
    while True:
        ws = None
        try:
            if os.getenv("LIVE_WS_ENABLED", "true").strip().lower() not in {"1", "true", "yes", "y", "on"}:
                _set_ws_state("DISABLED", subscribed=0, channels=[], error=None)
                time.sleep(5)
                continue
            info = market_session_now()
            summary = load_latest()
            if not bool(info.get("scan_allowed")):
                _set_ws_state("WAITING_SESSION", subscribed=0, channels=[], error=None)
                time.sleep(2)
                continue
            venue = str(info.get("venue") or "NXT").upper()
            # 중요: 현재 세션의 판정 결과 파일이 아직 없어도 WS는 즉시 연결한다.
            # 직전 후보 또는 LS master probe 종목을 현재 venue 채널로 구독하고,
            # 판정이 끝나 대상이 바뀌면 아래 signature 비교로 자동 재구독한다.
            subs = _ws_subscriptions(summary, venue)
            if not subs:
                _set_ws_state("WAITING_TARGETS", subscribed=0, channels=[], error=None)
                time.sleep(2)
                continue
            engine = get_engine()
            engine.ls._ensure_token()
            token = engine.ls.token
            ws = websocket.create_connection(WS_URL, timeout=5, enable_multithread=True)
            ws.settimeout(2)
            channels = sorted(set(x[0] for x in subs))
            for tr_cd, code in subs:
                ws.send(json.dumps({
                    "header": {"token": token, "tr_type": "3"},
                    "body": {"tr_cd": tr_cd, "tr_key": code},
                }, ensure_ascii=False))
            signature = (venue, tuple(subs))
            _set_ws_state("CONNECTED", subscribed=len(subs), channels=channels, error=None, connected=True)
            connected_mono = time.monotonic()
            last_tick_mono = 0.0
            first_frame_logged = False
            log.info("WS CONNECTED venue=%s channels=%s subscribed=%s", venue, channels, len(subs))
            while True:
                # 후보/세션이 바뀌면 재연결 후 신규 구독 집합으로 교체한다.
                info2 = market_session_now()
                summary2 = load_latest()
                venue2 = str(info2.get("venue") or venue).upper()
                subs2 = _ws_subscriptions(summary2, venue2) if bool(info2.get("scan_allowed")) else []
                if (venue2, tuple(subs2)) != signature:
                    break
                try:
                    raw = ws.recv()
                except websocket.WebSocketTimeoutException:
                    # 소켓만 연결되고 실제 체결 틱이 없는 상태를 CONNECTED로 오인하지 않는다.
                    now_mono = time.monotonic()
                    age = now_mono - (last_tick_mono or connected_mono)
                    try:
                        ws.ping()
                    except Exception:
                        raise RuntimeError("LS WebSocket ping failed")
                    if age >= WS_STALE_SECONDS:
                        _set_ws_state("STALE_NO_TICK", error=f"no real-time tick for {age:.1f}s")
                        raise RuntimeError(f"LS WebSocket no real-time tick for {age:.1f}s")
                    elif last_tick_mono == 0.0 and age >= 3.0:
                        _set_ws_state("CONNECTED_NO_TICK", error=None)
                    continue
                if raw in (None, ""):
                    raise RuntimeError("LS WebSocket closed")
                try:
                    payload = json.loads(raw)
                except Exception:
                    continue
                # 구독 승인/거절 응답을 header/body 어디에 오더라도 확인한다.
                flat_all = _flatten_dict(payload)
                hdr = payload.get("header") if isinstance(payload, dict) and isinstance(payload.get("header"), dict) else {}
                rsp_cd = str(hdr.get("rsp_cd") or flat_all.get("rsp_cd") or "").strip()
                rsp_msg = str(hdr.get("rsp_msg") or flat_all.get("rsp_msg") or "").strip()
                if rsp_cd and rsp_cd not in {"00000", "0"}:
                    log.warning("WS subscription response rsp_cd=%s msg=%s", rsp_cd, rsp_msg[:160])
                if not first_frame_logged:
                    log.info("WS FIRST_FRAME header_tr=%s rsp_cd=%s body_keys=%s", str(hdr.get("tr_cd") or ""), rsp_cd or "-", list((payload.get("body") or {}).keys())[:12] if isinstance(payload.get("body"), dict) else [])
                    first_frame_logged = True
                code, q, tr_cd = _extract_ws_quote(payload)
                if code and q:
                    last_tick_mono = time.monotonic()
                    _set_live_quote(code, q, f"WS:{tr_cd or 'REAL'}", venue)
                    _set_ws_state("RECEIVING", last_message=True, tr_cd=tr_cd or "REAL", symbol=code, tick=True)
                    with LOCK:
                        tick_count = int(STATE.get("ws_tick_count") or 0)
                    if tick_count <= 3 or tick_count % 100 == 0:
                        log.info("WS TICK count=%s tr=%s symbol=%s price=%s diff=%s", tick_count, tr_cd or "REAL", code, q.get("price"), q.get("diff"))
        except Exception as e:
            _set_ws_state("STALE", error=str(e)[:240])
            log.warning("WS reconnect: %s", e)
            time.sleep(2)
        finally:
            if ws is not None:
                try:
                    ws.close()
                except Exception:
                    pass


def _safe_float(v, default=0.0):
    try:
        x = float(v)
        return x if x == x else default
    except Exception:
        return default

def _live_rank_score(row):
    """기존 엔진 점수를 보존하면서 실시간 가격 HOLD/과열만 소폭 반영한다."""
    base = _safe_float(row.get("integrated_score"), 0.0)
    cur = _safe_float(row.get("current_price"), 0.0)
    ent = _safe_float(row.get("entry_price"), 0.0)
    day = _safe_float(row.get("change_pct"), 0.0)
    move = ((cur / ent) - 1.0) * 100.0 if cur > 0 and ent > 0 else 0.0
    # 숨은진주 목적에 맞게 진입가 위의 완만한 상승은 가점, 이탈/과열은 감점.
    adj = max(-6.0, min(6.0, move * 2.0))
    if move < -2.0:
        adj -= min(5.0, abs(move + 2.0) * 1.5)
    if day > 5.0:
        adj -= min(8.0, (day - 5.0) * 1.5)
    if day < -3.0:
        adj -= min(5.0, abs(day + 3.0))
    return round(base + adj, 3), round(move, 3)

def _rerank_live_rows(rows):
    ranked = []
    for r in rows or []:
        z = dict(r)
        score, move = _live_rank_score(z)
        z["live_rank_score"] = score
        z["move_from_entry_pct"] = move
        ranked.append(z)
    ranked.sort(key=lambda z: (
        _safe_float(z.get("live_rank_score"), -9999),
        _safe_float(z.get("deep_score"), -9999),
        _safe_float(z.get("integrated_score"), -9999),
    ), reverse=True)
    for i, z in enumerate(ranked, 1):
        z["live_rank"] = i
    return ranked

def _apply_live_overlay(summary):
    if not isinstance(summary, dict):
        return summary
    out = json.loads(json.dumps(summary, ensure_ascii=False, default=str))
    with LIVE_LOCK:
        qmap = dict(LIVE_QUOTES)
        meta = {k: dict(v) for k, v in LIVE_QUOTE_META.items()}
        updated = LIVE_UPDATED_KST
    sources = set()
    for key in ("top_final", "top_watch", "selection_history"):
        for r in out.get(key) or []:
            c = str(r.get("symbol", "")).zfill(6)
            q = qmap.get(c)
            if not q:
                continue
            m = meta.get(c) or {}
            if str(m.get("venue") or out.get("venue") or "").upper() != str(out.get("venue") or "").upper():
                continue
            px = _quote_price(q)
            pct = _quote_pct(q)
            if px is not None:
                r["current_price"] = px
            if pct is not None:
                r["change_pct"] = pct
            src = str(m.get("source") or q.get("_source") or "LIVE")
            sources.add(src)
            r["live_quote"] = True
            r["live_source"] = src
            r["live_updated_kst"] = m.get("updated_kst")
    # 화면 순위는 저장 순서가 아니라 최신 현재가를 반영해 매 응답마다 재계산한다.
    out["top_final"] = _rerank_live_rows(out.get("top_final") or [])
    out["top_watch"] = _rerank_live_rows(out.get("top_watch") or [])
    out["live_updated_kst"] = updated
    out["live_quote_count"] = len(qmap)
    out["live_price_source"] = ",".join(sorted(sources)) if sources else "WAITING"
    with LOCK:
        out["ws_status"] = STATE.get("ws_status")
        out["ws_connected_kst"] = STATE.get("ws_connected_kst")
        out["ws_last_message_kst"] = STATE.get("ws_last_message_kst")
        out["ws_last_tr_cd"] = STATE.get("ws_last_tr_cd")
        out["ws_last_symbol"] = STATE.get("ws_last_symbol")
        out["ws_tick_count"] = STATE.get("ws_tick_count")
        out["ws_subscribed"] = STATE.get("ws_subscribed")
        out["ws_channels"] = STATE.get("ws_channels")
        out["rest_fallback_active"] = STATE.get("rest_fallback_active")
    return out


def live_quote_loop():
    """WebSocket 누락/장애 종목만 REST로 보완하는 fallback 루프."""
    while True:
        fallback_used = False
        try:
            summary = load_latest()
            info = market_session_now()
            if bool(info.get("scan_allowed")):
                venue = str(info.get("venue") or "KRX").upper()
                targets = _ws_target_map(summary, venue)
                stale = [c for c in targets if not _quote_is_fresh(c)]
                if stale:
                    # 딥스캔 중에는 REST 호출을 양보하되 WebSocket 수신은 계속된다.
                    with LOCK:
                        busy = bool(STATE.get("running"))
                    if not busy:
                        engine = get_engine()
                        engine.fast_history.set_venue(venue, "N" if venue == "NXT" else "K")
                        qmap = engine.fast_history.multi_quotes(stale)
                        for code, q in qmap.items():
                            _set_live_quote(code, q, "REST:t8450" if venue == "NXT" else "REST:t8407", venue)
                        fallback_used = bool(qmap)
            _set_ws_state(STATE.get("ws_status", "WAITING"), rest_fallback=fallback_used)
        except Exception as e:
            logging.getLogger("alpha500_live").warning("REST fallback failed: %s", e)
            _set_ws_state(STATE.get("ws_status", "STALE"), rest_fallback=False)
        time.sleep(REST_FALLBACK_SECONDS)

def run_scan(session_info=None):
    info = dict(session_info or market_session_now())
    if not bool(info.get("scan_allowed")):
        with LOCK:
            STATE["venue"] = info.get("venue", "NXT")
            STATE["session"] = info.get("session", "WAITING_NXT")
            if not STATE["running"]:
                STATE["status"] = "WAITING"
                STATE["phase"] = info.get("session", "WAITING")
                STATE["progress_current"] = 0
                STATE["progress_total"] = 0
                STATE["progress_message"] = "다음 거래 세션 대기 중"
        return False
    with LOCK:
        if STATE["running"]:
            return False
        STATE["running"] = True
        STATE["venue"] = info.get("venue", "KRX")
        STATE["session"] = info.get("session", "KRX_REGULAR")
        STATE["status"] = "SCANNING"
        STATE["last_started"] = now_kst_iso()
        STATE["last_error"] = None
        STATE["phase"] = "UNIVERSE"
        STATE["progress_current"] = 0
        STATE["progress_total"] = 500
        STATE["progress_message"] = f"{STATE['session']} · {STATE['venue']} 유니버스 구성 중"
    try:
        engine = get_engine()
        *_, final, summary = engine.run_integrated(bootstrap_missing=True, session_info=info)
        with LOCK:
            STATE["last_summary"] = summary
            STATE["venue"] = summary.get("venue", STATE["venue"])
            STATE["session"] = summary.get("session", STATE["session"])
            STATE["status"] = "READY"
            STATE["last_finished"] = summary.get("timestamp_kst") or now_kst_iso()
            STATE["phase"] = "COMPLETE"
            STATE["progress_current"] = 1
            STATE["progress_total"] = 1
            STATE["progress_message"] = f"{STATE['session']} 완료 · FINAL {int(summary.get('final_count',0))} · WATCH {len(summary.get('top_watch') or [])}"
    except Exception as e:
        with LOCK:
            STATE["status"] = "ERROR"
            STATE["last_error"] = f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"
            STATE["last_finished"] = now_kst_iso()
            STATE["phase"] = "ERROR"
            STATE["progress_message"] = STATE["last_error"]
        traceback.print_exc()
    finally:
        with LOCK:
            STATE["running"] = False
    return True


def scan_loop():
    initial = int(os.getenv("INITIAL_SCAN_DELAY_SECONDS", "3"))
    interval = int(os.getenv("SCAN_INTERVAL_SECONDS", "3600"))
    poll = int(os.getenv("SESSION_POLL_SECONDS", "15"))
    time.sleep(max(0, initial))
    last_session = None
    last_run = 0.0
    while True:
        info = market_session_now()
        session_key = str(info.get("session"))
        with LOCK:
            STATE["venue"] = info.get("venue", "NXT")
            STATE["session"] = session_key
            busy = STATE["running"]
            if not info.get("scan_allowed") and not busy:
                STATE["status"] = "WAITING" if STATE.get("last_finished") is None else "READY"
                STATE["phase"] = session_key
                STATE["progress_message"] = "NXT 08:00 개장 대기" if session_key == "WAITING_NXT" else "세션 전환 대기"
        due = bool(info.get("scan_allowed")) and (session_key != last_session or time.monotonic() - last_run >= max(60, interval))
        if due and not busy:
            if run_scan(info):
                last_run = time.monotonic()
                last_session = session_key
        time.sleep(max(5, poll))


def state_copy():
    with LOCK:
        d = dict(STATE)
    d["ls_configured"] = bool(os.getenv("LS_APP_KEY") and os.getenv("LS_APP_SECRET"))
    d["master"] = MASTER
    d["now_kst"] = now_kst_iso()
    live = market_session_now()
    d["expected_venue"] = live.get("venue")
    d["expected_session"] = live.get("session")
    total = int(d.get("progress_total") or 0)
    cur = int(d.get("progress_current") or 0)
    d["progress_pct"] = round(cur / total * 100, 1) if total > 0 else 0.0
    return d


class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    def _json(self, status, obj):
        body = json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default=str).encode("utf-8")
        self.send_response(status)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8")
        self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store, no-cache, must-revalidate")
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
        self.end_headers()
        if self.command != "HEAD":
            self.wfile.write(body)

    def _html(self, status, text):
        body = text.encode("utf-8")
        self.send_response(status)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", "text/html; charset=utf-8")
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
        self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store, no-cache, must-revalidate")
        self.end_headers()
        if self.command != "HEAD":
            self.wfile.write(body)

<div class="section"><h2>판정 요약</h2><div id="summary" class="summary"></div></div>

const esc=v=>String(v??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,m=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[m]));
const num=(v,d=0)=>{const n=Number(v);return Number.isFinite(n)?n.toLocaleString('ko-KR',{minimumFractionDigits:d,maximumFractionDigits:d}):'-'};
const pct=v=>{const n=Number(v);return Number.isFinite(n)?((n>0?'+':'')+n.toFixed(2)+'%'):'-'};
const cls=v=>Number(v)>0?'pos':Number(v)<0?'neg':'';
const box=(k,v)=>`<div class="sbox"><div class="k">${esc(k)}</div><div class="snum">${esc(v)}</div></div>`;
function candidateRows(rows){if(!rows?.length)return '<tr><td colspan="14" class="empty left">현재 표시할 후보가 없습니다.</td></tr>';return rows.map((r,i)=>`<tr><td>${num(r.live_rank||i+1)}</td><td class="left"><b>${esc(r.name)}</b></td><td>${num(r.current_price)}</td><td class="${cls(r.change_pct)}">${pct(r.change_pct)}</td><td>${num(r.entry_price)}</td><td class="${cls(r.move_from_entry_pct)}">${pct(r.move_from_entry_pct)}</td><td>${num(r.live_rank_score,1)}</td><td>${num(r.integrated_score,1)}</td><td>${num(r.deep_score,1)}</td><td><span class="badge">${esc(r.deep_grade)}</span></td><td class="left">${esc(r.MEGA_SECTOR)}</td><td class="left">${esc(r.ENERGY_CLUSTER)}</td><td>${num(r.cluster_energy,1)}</td><td>${esc(r.cluster_state)}</td></tr>`).join('')}
function sectorRows(rows){if(!rows?.length)return '<tr><td colspan="11" class="empty left">섹터 에너지 데이터 준비 중</td></tr>';return rows.slice(0,30).map(r=>{let mv=esc(r.movement);let mc=String(r.movement||'').startsWith('↑')?'up':String(r.movement||'').startsWith('↓')?'down':'';return `<tr><td>${num(r.rank)}</td><td class="left"><b>${esc(r.ENERGY_CLUSTER)}</b></td><td>${esc(r.cluster_state)}</td><td>${num(r.cluster_energy,1)}</td><td class="${cls(r.energy_delta)}">${r.energy_delta==null?'-':(Number(r.energy_delta)>0?'+':'')+num(r.energy_delta,1)}</td><td class="${mc}">${mv}</td><td>${pct(Number(r.cluster_breadth)*100)}</td><td class="${cls(r.cluster_avg_return)}">${pct(r.cluster_avg_return)}</td><td class="${cls(r.cluster_market_rs)}">${num(r.cluster_market_rs,2)}</td><td>${num(r.cluster_turnover_accel,2)}</td><td>${num(r.cluster_members)}</td></tr>`}).join('')}
function historyRows(rows){if(!rows?.length)return '<tr><td colspan="14" class="empty left">아직 선정 히스토리가 없습니다.</td></tr>';return rows.slice(0,200).map(r=>`<tr><td class="left">${esc(r.selected_at_kst||'-')}</td><td>${esc(r.venue||'-')}</td><td>${esc(r.session||'-')}</td><td>${num(r.occurrence)}</td><td>${esc(r.selection_type)}</td><td class="left"><b>${esc(r.name)}</b></td><td>${num(r.current_price)}</td><td class="${cls(r.change_pct)}">${pct(r.change_pct)}</td><td>${num(r.entry_price)}</td><td>${num(r.deep_score,1)}</td><td>${esc(r.deep_grade)}</td><td class="left">${esc(r.ENERGY_CLUSTER)}</td><td>${num(r.cluster_energy,1)}</td><td>${esc(r.cluster_state)}</td></tr>`).join('')}

async function runScan(){try{const z=await fetch('/api/run',{method:'POST',cache:'no-store'});if(z.status===409)alert('이미 판정이 실행 중입니다.');refresh()}catch(e){alert(e)}}

        self._html(200, page)

    def do_HEAD(self):
        path = urlparse(self.path).path
        if path in {"/", "/health", "/api/status", "/api/results"}:
            if path == "/":
                return self._dashboard()
            self.send_response(200); self.send_header("Content-Length", "0"); self.end_headers(); return
        self.send_response(404); self.end_headers()

    def do_GET(self):
        path = urlparse(self.path).path
        if path == "/":
            return self._dashboard()
        if path == "/health":
            s = state_copy()
            return self._json(200, {"ok": True, "version": VERSION, "provider": "LS", "venue": s.get("venue"), "session": s.get("session"), "status": s["status"], "running": s["running"], "ls_configured": s["ls_configured"], "now_kst": s["now_kst"]})
        if path == "/api/status":
            return self._json(200, state_copy())
        if path == "/api/results":
            latest = load_latest()
            return self._json(200, _apply_live_overlay(latest) if latest is not None else {"status": "NO_RESULT_YET", **state_copy()})
        if path == "/api/run":
            with LOCK: busy = STATE["running"]
            ok = False
            if not busy:
                threading.Thread(target=run_scan, daemon=True).start(); ok = True
            return self._json(202 if ok else 409, {"accepted": ok, "state": state_copy()})
        return self._json(404, {"error": "not found"})

    def do_POST(self):
        if urlparse(self.path).path == "/api/run":
            return self.do_GET()
        return self._json(404, {"error": "not found"})

    def log_message(self, fmt, *args):
        print(f"WEB {self.address_string()} {fmt % args}")


def main():
    latest = load_latest()
    with LOCK:
        if latest:
            STATE["status"] = "READY"
            STATE["last_summary"] = latest
            STATE["last_finished"] = latest.get("timestamp_kst")
            STATE["venue"] = latest.get("venue", STATE.get("venue"))
            STATE["session"] = latest.get("session", STATE.get("session"))
            STATE["phase"] = "COMPLETE"
            STATE["progress_current"] = 1
            STATE["progress_total"] = 1
            STATE["progress_message"] = "직전 판정 결과 표시 중"
        else:
            STATE["status"] = "IDLE"
            STATE["phase"] = "IDLE"
            STATE["progress_message"] = "첫 판정 대기"
    if os.getenv("AUTO_SCAN", "true").strip().lower() in {"1", "true", "yes", "y", "on"}:
        threading.Thread(target=scan_loop, daemon=True).start()
    threading.Thread(target=live_ws_loop, daemon=True).start()
    threading.Thread(target=live_quote_loop, daemon=True).start()
    server = ThreadingHTTPServer(("0.0.0.0", PORT), Handler)
    print(json.dumps({"event": "server_listening", "version": VERSION, "port": PORT, "kst": now_kst_iso()}, ensure_ascii=False))
    server.serve_forever()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
전체시장_2단계_딥스캔_V1_1.py

V1.1 핵심
=========
[1차] KOSPI/KOSDAQ 전체시장 가격구조 전수검색
      - 거래대금 상위 랭킹을 출발점으로 사용하지 않음
      - Alpha Heikin-Ashi / Fast HA / VWAP / EMA / RSI / RVOL / 압축 구조
      - Stage1 점수로 30~50개만 압축

[2차] 압축 후보에만 Deep Data 호출
      - 체결강도
      - 외국인/기관
      - 프로그램
      - 섹터 breadth
      - 실제 데이터 커버리지/신선도
      - 최종 Deep Score 재계산

데이터 진실성 원칙
=================
- KRX 전용. NXT/통합 데이터와 혼합하지 않음.
- OHLCV 봉은 한 종목/한 판정에서 단일 가격 공급자로 완결.
- Deep Feature는 서로 다른 증권사에서 받을 수 있으나,
  각 feature의 source를 별도 기록하며 venue는 KRX만 허용.
- KIS 종목별 투자자 데이터는 장중 실시간으로 간주하지 않음.
- LS가 연결되어 있으면 장중 Deep Data는 LS를 우선 사용.
- 누락 데이터를 0으로 위장하지 않고 coverage를 낮춰 감점.
- 섹터 breadth는 KIS 마스터의 업종분류 + 전체시장 당일 등락을
  이용하여 계산. 외부 sector API가 없어도 전체시장으로 직접 계산.

환경변수
========
DEEP_CANDIDATES=40
FINAL_TOP_N=20
STAGE1_MIN_SCORE=48
DEEP_PROVIDER=auto       # auto|ls|kis
DEEP_FAIL_OPEN=true      # Deep 일부 누락 시 탈락 대신 coverage 감점
LS_KRX_EXCHGUBUN=K
SECTOR_MIN_MEMBERS=4

기존 V1.0 환경변수(KIS_APP_KEY, LS_APP_KEY 등)는 그대로 사용.
"""

def env_bool(name: str, default: bool) -> bool:
    v = os.getenv(name)
    if v is None:
        return default
    return v.strip().lower() in {"1", "true", "yes", "y", "on"}


def fnum(v: Any, default: float = 0.0) -> float:
    try:
        if v in (None, "", " "):
            return default
        x = float(str(v).replace(",", ""))
        if math.isfinite(x):
            return x
    except Exception:
        pass
    return default


def clip100(v: float) -> float:
    return float(np.clip(v, 0.0, 100.0))


def norm(v: float, lo: float, hi: float) -> float:
    if hi <= lo:
        return 0.0
    return float(np.clip((v - lo) / (hi - lo), 0.0, 1.0))


@dataclass
class DeepMetrics:
    symbol: str
    venue: str = "KRX"

    trade_strength: Optional[float] = None
    trade_strength_ma5: Optional[float] = None
    trade_strength_ma20: Optional[float] = None

    foreign_net: Optional[float] = None
    institution_net: Optional[float] = None
    foreign_3d: Optional[float] = None
    institution_3d: Optional[float] = None
    flow_volume_base: Optional[float] = None

    program_net: Optional[float] = None
    program_buy: Optional[float] = None
    program_sell: Optional[float] = None

    sector_code: Optional[str] = None
    sector_breadth: Optional[float] = None
    sector_avg_return: Optional[float] = None
    sector_member_count: Optional[int] = None

    source_strength: Optional[str] = None
    source_flow: Optional[str] = None
    source_program: Optional[str] = None
    source_sector: Optional[str] = None

    observed_at: Optional[str] = None
    errors: Optional[List[str]] = None

    def coverage(self) -> float:
        groups = [
            self.trade_strength is not None,
            self.foreign_net is not None or self.institution_net is not None,
            self.program_net is not None,
            self.sector_breadth is not None,
        ]
        return sum(groups) / len(groups)

    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        d = asdict(self)
        d["deep_coverage"] = round(self.coverage(), 3)
        return d

    ]
    KOSDAQ_COLS = [
        '증권그룹구분코드','시가총액규모','지수업종대분류','지수업종중분류','지수업종소분류',
        '벤처','저유동성','KRX종목','ETP','KRX100','KRX자동차','KRX반도체','KRX바이오','KRX은행',
        'SPAC','KRX에너지화학','KRX철강','단기과열','KRX미디어통신','KRX건설','투자주의환기',
        'KRX증권','KRX선박','KRX섹터보험','KRX섹터운송','KOSDAQ150','기준가','매매수량단위',
        '시간외수량단위','거래정지','정리매매','관리종목','시장경고','경고예고','불성실공시',
        '우회상장','락구분','액면변경','증자구분','증거금비율','신용가능','신용기간','전일거래량',
        '액면가','상장일자','상장주수','자본금','결산월','공모가','우선주','공매도과열','이상급등',
        'KRX300','매출액','영업이익','경상이익','당기순이익','ROE','기준년월','시가총액',
        '그룹사코드','회사신용한도초과','담보대출가능','대주가능'
    ]

    def __init__(self, timeout: float = 20.0):
        self.timeout = timeout
        self.s = requests.Session()
        self.map: Dict[str, Dict[str, str]] = {}

    @staticmethod
    def _decode_zip(content: bytes) -> str:
        with zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(content)) as z:
            raw = z.read(z.namelist()[0])
        return raw.decode("cp949", errors="ignore")

    @staticmethod
    def _parse_text(text: str, tail_len: int, widths: List[int], cols: List[str], market: str):
        rows = []
        for line in text.splitlines():
            if len(line) <= tail_len:
                continue
            head = line[:-tail_len]
            tail = line[-tail_len:]
            code = head[0:9].strip()
            if len(code) > 6:
                code = code[-6:]
            if not re.fullmatch(r"\d{6}", code):
                continue
            name = head[21:].strip()

            vals = []
            p = 0
            for w in widths:
                vals.append(tail[p:p+w].strip())
                p += w
            meta = dict(zip(cols, vals))
            rows.append({
                "symbol": code,
                "name": name,
                "market": market,
                "sector_large": meta.get("지수업종대분류", ""),
                "sector_mid": meta.get("지수업종중분류", ""),
                "sector_small": meta.get("지수업종소분류", ""),
            })
        return rows

    def load(self):
        rows = []
        for market, url, tail, widths, cols in [
            ("KOSPI", self.KOSPI_URL, 228, self.KOSPI_WIDTHS, self.KOSPI_COLS),
            ("KOSDAQ", self.KOSDAQ_URL, 222, self.KOSDAQ_WIDTHS, self.KOSDAQ_COLS),
        ]:
            try:
                r = self.s.get(url, timeout=self.timeout)
                r.raise_for_status()
                text = self._decode_zip(r.content)
                rows.extend(self._parse_text(text, tail, widths, cols, market))
            except Exception as e:
                LOG.warning("sector master load failed %s: %s", market, e)

        self.map = {r["symbol"]: r for r in rows}
        return self.map

    def attach(self, stage1: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
        x = stage1.copy()
        if not self.map:
            self.load()
        if not self.map:
            x["sector_code"] = x["market"].astype(str)
            x["sector_level"] = "MARKET_PROXY"
            return x

        def sec(code):
            m = self.map.get(str(code).zfill(6), {})
            # 소분류 -> 중분류 -> 대분류 순으로 가장 세밀한 유효 코드를 사용
            for key in ("sector_small", "sector_mid", "sector_large"):
                v = str(m.get(key, "")).strip()
                if v and v not in {"0000", "0"}:
                    return f'{m.get("market","")}-{v}'
            return f'{m.get("market","UNKNOWN")}-UNKNOWN'

        x["sector_code"] = x["symbol"].astype(str).str.zfill(6).map(sec)
        x["sector_level"] = np.where(x["sector_code"].str.contains("UNKNOWN"), "MARKET_PROXY", "KIS_MASTER")
        return x

    @staticmethod
    def calculate_breadth(stage1: pd.DataFrame, min_members: int = 4) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, float]]:
        """
        latest close의 전일 대비 등락률이 있으면 그것으로 breadth 계산.
        stage1_current_return 컬럼이 없으면 score가 아니라 close/prior_close를 사용해야 하므로
        이 함수 호출 전에 current_return_pct를 만들어 둔다.
        """
        out: Dict[str, Dict[str, float]] = {}
        if "sector_code" not in stage1.columns or "current_return_pct" not in stage1.columns:
            return out

        valid = stage1.dropna(subset=["sector_code", "current_return_pct"])
        for sector, g in valid.groupby("sector_code"):
            if len(g) < min_members:
                continue
            r = pd.to_numeric(g["current_return_pct"], errors="coerce").dropna()
            if len(r) < min_members:
                continue
            adv = float((r > 0).mean())
            strong = float((r > 1.0).mean())
            weak = float((r < -1.0).mean())
            breadth = np.clip(0.70 * adv + 0.20 * strong + 0.10 * (1.0 - weak), 0, 1)
            out[str(sector)] = {
                "breadth": float(breadth),
                "avg_return": float(r.mean()),
                "member_count": int(len(r)),
            }
        return out


class LSDeepProvider:
    """LS 거래소분리 Deep feature 수집기. KRX(K)/NXT(N)를 세션별로 절대 혼합하지 않는다."""

    name = "LS"

    def __init__(self, ls: Optional[LSProvider] = None):
        self.ls = ls or LSProvider()
        self.exchgubun = os.getenv("LS_ACTIVE_EXCHGUBUN", os.getenv("LS_KRX_EXCHGUBUN", "K")).strip() or "K"
        self.venue = "NXT" if self.exchgubun == "N" else "KRX"

    def set_exchange(self, exchgubun: str, venue: Optional[str] = None):
        x = str(exchgubun or "K").upper()
        if x not in {"K", "N"}:
            raise ValueError(f"unsupported exchange={x}")
        self.exchgubun = x
        self.venue = str(venue or ("NXT" if x == "N" else "KRX")).upper()

    @property
    def configured(self) -> bool:
        return self.ls.configured

    def trade_strength(self, symbol: str) -> Dict[str, float]:
        # t8454는 거래소구분을 명시할 수 있어 NXT/KRX 공통으로 사용한다.
        now = datetime.now().strftime("%H%M")
        body = {
            "t8454InBlock": {
                "shcode": symbol,
                "cvolume": 0,
                "starttime": "",
                "endtime": now,
                "cts_time": "",
                "exchgubun": self.exchgubun,
            }
        }
        r = self.ls._post_tr(self.ls.market_path, "t8454", body)
        rows = r.json().get("t8454OutBlock1") or []
        if not rows:
            raise BrokerError(f"LS t8454 empty {self.venue}")
        rows = sorted(rows, key=lambda z: str(z.get("chetime", "")), reverse=True)
        row = rows[0]
        return {
            "trade_strength": fnum(row.get("chdegree"), None),
            "trade_strength_ma5": None,
            "trade_strength_ma20": None,
        }

    def investor_flow(self, symbol: str) -> Dict[str, float]:
        end = date.today()
        start = end - timedelta(days=14)
        body = {
            "t1702InBlock": {
                "fromdt": start.strftime("%Y%m%d"),
                "todt": end.strftime("%Y%m%d"),
                "shcode": symbol,
                "volvalgb": "1",   # 수량
                "msmdgb": "0",     # 순매수
                "gubun": "0",      # 일간
                "exchgubun": self.exchgubun,
            }
        }
        r = self.ls._post_tr("/stock/frgr-itt", "t1702", body)
        rows = r.json().get("t1702OutBlock1") or []
        if not rows:
            raise BrokerError("LS t1702 empty")
        rows = sorted(rows, key=lambda z: str(z.get("date", "")), reverse=True)
        latest = rows[0]
        recent3 = rows[:3]

        foreign = fnum(latest.get("tjj0016"), 0.0)
        inst = fnum(latest.get("tjj0018"), 0.0)
        vol = abs(fnum(latest.get("volume"), 0.0))

        return {
            "foreign_net": foreign,
            "institution_net": inst,
            "foreign_3d": sum(fnum(z.get("tjj0016"), 0.0) for z in recent3),
            "institution_3d": sum(fnum(z.get("tjj0018"), 0.0) for z in recent3),
            "flow_volume_base": vol,
        }

    def program_flow(self, symbol: str) -> Dict[str, float]:
        today = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")
        now = datetime.now().strftime("%H%M%S")
        body = {
            "t1637InBlock": {
                "gubun1": "0",       # 수량
                "gubun2": "0",       # 시간
                "shcode": symbol,
                "date": today,
                "time": now,
                "cts_idx": 9999,
                "exchgubun": self.exchgubun,
            }
        }
        r = self.ls._post_tr("/stock/program", "t1637", body)
        rows = r.json().get("t1637OutBlock1") or []
        if not rows:
            raise BrokerError("LS t1637 empty")
        rows = sorted(rows, key=lambda z: (str(z.get("date", "")), str(z.get("time", ""))), reverse=True)
        row = rows[0]
        return {
            "program_net": fnum(row.get("svolume"), None),
            "program_buy": fnum(row.get("stksvolume"), None),
            "program_sell": fnum(row.get("offervolume"), None),
        }

    def collect(self, symbol: str) -> DeepMetrics:
        d = DeepMetrics(symbol=symbol, errors=[])
        try:
            d.__dict__.update(self.trade_strength(symbol))
            d.source_strength = f"LS:t8454:{self.venue}"
        except Exception as e:
            d.errors.append(f"strength:{e}")

        try:
            d.__dict__.update(self.investor_flow(symbol))
            d.source_flow = f"LS:t1702:{self.venue}:NON_REALTIME"
        except Exception as e:
            d.errors.append(f"flow:{e}")

        try:
            d.__dict__.update(self.program_flow(symbol))
            d.source_program = f"LS:t1637:{self.venue}"
        except Exception as e:
            d.errors.append(f"program:{e}")

        d.observed_at = datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
        return d


class KISDeepProvider:
    """
    KIS Deep 보완:
    - 체결강도: inquire-ccnl FHKST01010300 (KRX J)
    - 투자자: inquire_investor는 공식 설명상 당일 데이터가 장 종료 후 제공되므로
      V1.1 장중 Deep Score에는 사용하지 않는다.
    """

    name = "KIS"

    def __init__(self, kis: Optional[KISProvider] = None):
        self.kis = kis or KISProvider()

    @property
    def configured(self) -> bool:
        return self.kis.configured

    def trade_strength(self, symbol: str) -> Dict[str, float]:
        self.kis.rl.wait()
        r = self.kis.s.get(
            self.kis.base + "/uapi/domestic-stock/v1/quotations/inquire-ccnl",
            headers=self.kis._headers("FHKST01010300"),
            params={"FID_COND_MRKT_DIV_CODE": "J", "FID_INPUT_ISCD": symbol},
            timeout=self.kis.timeout,
        )
        if r.status_code != 200:
            raise BrokerError(f"KIS ccnl HTTP {r.status_code}")
        j = r.json()
        rows = j.get("output") or []
        if isinstance(rows, dict):
            rows = [rows]
        if not rows:
            raise BrokerError("KIS inquire-ccnl empty")
        vals = [fnum(z.get("tday_rltv"), np.nan) for z in rows]
        vals = [v for v in vals if np.isfinite(v)]
        if not vals:
            raise BrokerError("KIS tday_rltv missing")
        return {"trade_strength": vals[0]}

    def collect(self, symbol: str) -> DeepMetrics:
        d = DeepMetrics(symbol=symbol, errors=[])
        try:
            d.__dict__.update(self.trade_strength(symbol))
            d.source_strength = "KIS:FHKST01010300:KRX"
        except Exception as e:
            d.errors.append(f"strength:{e}")
        d.observed_at = datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
        return d


class CompositeDeepProvider:
    """
    auto:
      LS -> 체결강도/수급/프로그램
      누락 체결강도만 KIS 보완

    가격 봉과 Deep feature의 공급자가 달라도 각 source를 기록하며
    venue=KRX가 아닌 데이터는 본 엔진에 들어오지 않게 한다.
    """

    def __init__(self, ls: LSDeepProvider, kis: KISDeepProvider, mode: str = "auto"):
        self.ls = ls
        self.kis = kis
        self.mode = mode

    def collect(self, symbol: str) -> DeepMetrics:
        if self.mode == "ls":
            return self.ls.collect(symbol)
        if self.mode == "kis":
            return self.kis.collect(symbol)

        if self.ls.configured:
            d = self.ls.collect(symbol)
        else:
            d = DeepMetrics(symbol=symbol, errors=["LS:NOT_CONFIGURED"])

        if d.trade_strength is None and self.kis.configured:
            k = self.kis.collect(symbol)
            if k.trade_strength is not None:
                d.trade_strength = k.trade_strength
                d.source_strength = k.source_strength
            if k.errors:
                d.errors.extend(k.errors)

        if d.observed_at is None:
            d.observed_at = datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
        return d


class TwoStageDeepScanner:
    def __init__(self):
        self.market = MarketScanner()
        self.deep_n = env_int("DEEP_CANDIDATES", 40)
        self.final_top_n = env_int("FINAL_TOP_N", 20)
        self.stage1_min_score = env_float("STAGE1_MIN_SCORE", 48.0)
        self.sector_min_members = env_int("SECTOR_MIN_MEMBERS", 4)
        self.fail_open = env_bool("DEEP_FAIL_OPEN", True)
        self.deep_mode = os.getenv("DEEP_PROVIDER", "auto").lower().strip()
        self.sector = SectorResolver(timeout=env_float("REQUEST_TIMEOUT", 12.0))
        self.deep = CompositeDeepProvider(
            LSDeepProvider(self.market.ls),
            KISDeepProvider(self.market.kis),
            self.deep_mode,
        )
        self.result_dir = self.market.result_dir

    @staticmethod
    def _stage1_state_bonus(state: str) -> float:
        return {
            "UP_START": 12.0,
            "REVERSAL_ARMED": 11.0,
            "DOWN_WEAKEN": 5.0,
            "UP_TREND": 3.0,
            "UP_EXHAUST": -8.0,
            "DOWN": -12.0,
            "ERROR": -50.0,
        }.get(state, 0.0)

    def stage1_pre_score(self, df: pd.DataFrame, symbol: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        e = self.market.engine.enrich(df)
        r = e.iloc[-1]
        state = self.market.engine._state(e)

        # 1차에서는 외인/기관/프로그램/sector score를 의도적으로 사용하지 않는다.
        # 그래야 Deep Data가 없는 종목도 동일 기준으로 비교된다.
        fast_up = bool(r["fast_up"])
        alpha_up = bool(r["alpha_up"])
        alpha_slope = fnum(r["alpha_slope"])
        body = fnum(r["body_atr"])
        comp = fnum(r["body_compression"])
        lw = fnum(r["lower_wick_ratio"])
        uw = fnum(r["upper_wick_ratio"])
        rsi = fnum(r["rsi"], 50)
        rvol = fnum(r["rvol"], 1)
        close = fnum(r["close"])
        ema20 = max(1e-12, fnum(r["ema20"], close))
        atr = max(1e-12, fnum(r["atr"], close * 0.02))
        vwap = fnum(r["vwap"], close)

        ha_score = 0.0
        ha_score += 18 if fast_up else 0
        ha_score += 12 if alpha_up else 0
        ha_score += 8 if alpha_slope > 0 else 0

        reversal = 0.0
        reversal += 10 * norm(comp, 0.0, 0.45)
        reversal += 8 * norm(lw, 0.20, 0.60)
        if body <= 0.28:
            reversal += 5
        if state in {TrendState.REVERSAL_ARMED, TrendState.UP_START}:
            reversal += 7

        price_hold = 0.0
        if close >= vwap:
            price_hold += 10
        elif (vwap - close) / atr <= 0.35:
            price_hold += 5
        if close >= ema20:
            price_hold += 8

        momentum = 0.0
        if 42 <= rsi <= 62:
            momentum += 7
        elif 35 <= rsi < 42:
            momentum += 4
        elif rsi > 72:
            momentum -= 4
        momentum += 7 * norm(rvol, 0.85, 1.50)

        extension = (close - ema20) / atr
        extension_penalty = max(0.0, min(12.0, (extension - 2.0) * 4.0))

        score = (
            ha_score + reversal + price_hold + momentum
            + self._stage1_state_bonus(state.value)
            - extension_penalty
        )
        # 위 항목의 이론 최대가 100을 약간 넘으므로 0~100 제한
        score = clip100(score)

        prev_close = fnum(e["close"].iloc[-2], close)
        current_return_pct = (close / prev_close - 1.0) * 100 if prev_close else 0.0

        return {
            "symbol": symbol,
            "stage1_state": state.value,
            "stage1_score": round(score, 2),
            "current_return_pct": round(current_return_pct, 4),
            "stage1_rsi": round(rsi, 2),
            "stage1_rvol": round(rvol, 2),
            "stage1_body_atr": round(body, 4),
            "stage1_compression": round(comp, 4),
            "stage1_lower_wick": round(lw, 4),
            "stage1_upper_wick": round(uw, 4),
            "stage1_above_vwap": bool(close >= vwap),
            "stage1_ema20_hold": bool(close >= ema20),
            "stage1_close": close,
            "stage1_price_source": "",
        }

    def run_stage1(
        self,
        universe_csv: Optional[str] = None,
        common_only: bool = True,
        max_symbols: Optional[int] = None,
    ) -> Tuple[pd.DataFrame, Dict[str, pd.DataFrame]]:
        universe = self.market.get_universe(universe_csv, common_only=common_only)
        if max_symbols:
            universe = universe[:max_symbols]

        rows = []
        data_cache: Dict[str, pd.DataFrame] = {}
        total = len(universe)
        LOG.info("STAGE1 ZERO-BASE start universe=%d", total)

        for i, item in enumerate(universe, 1):
            try:
                bars, provider, trace = self.market.get_symbol_data(item.code)
                data_cache[item.code] = bars
                d = self.stage1_pre_score(bars, item.code)
                d.update({
                    "name": item.name,
                    "market": item.market,
                    "stage1_price_source": provider,
                    "stage1_fallback_trace": " | ".join(trace),
                })
                rows.append(d)
            except Exception as e:
                rows.append({
                    "symbol": item.code,
                    "name": item.name,
                    "market": item.market,
                    "stage1_state": "ERROR",
                    "stage1_score": 0.0,
                    "current_return_pct": np.nan,
                    "error": str(e)[:300],
                })

            if i % 50 == 0 or i == total:
                LOG.info("STAGE1 progress %d/%d", i, total)

        out = pd.DataFrame(rows)
        if out.empty:
            return out, data_cache

        out = self.sector.attach(out)
        breadth = self.sector.calculate_breadth(out, self.sector_min_members)

        out["sector_breadth"] = out["sector_code"].map(
            lambda s: breadth.get(str(s), {}).get("breadth", np.nan)
        )
        out["sector_avg_return"] = out["sector_code"].map(
            lambda s: breadth.get(str(s), {}).get("avg_return", np.nan)
        )
        out["sector_member_count"] = out["sector_code"].map(
            lambda s: breadth.get(str(s), {}).get("member_count", np.nan)
        )

        # ERROR 제외 후 상태 우선 + 점수 순
        state_priority = {
            "UP_START": 0,
            "REVERSAL_ARMED": 1,
            "DOWN_WEAKEN": 2,
            "UP_TREND": 3,
            "UP_EXHAUST": 4,
            "DOWN": 5,
            "ERROR": 9,
        }
        out["_p"] = out["stage1_state"].map(state_priority).fillna(8)
        out = out.sort_values(
            ["_p", "stage1_score", "sector_breadth"],
            ascending=[True, False, False],
            na_position="last",
        ).drop(columns="_p").reset_index(drop=True)
        return out, data_cache

    @staticmethod
    def strength_score(d: DeepMetrics) -> float:
        if d.trade_strength is None:
            return 50.0
        s = d.trade_strength
        base = 100 * norm(s, 80, 160)
        # 단순 절대강도보다 20일 평균 대비 회복/가속을 보너스
        if d.trade_strength_ma20 is not None:
            delta = s - d.trade_strength_ma20
            base += np.clip(delta * 0.35, -10, 10)
        return clip100(base)

    @staticmethod
    def flow_score(d: DeepMetrics) -> float:
        vals = []
        base_vol = max(1.0, abs(d.flow_volume_base or 0.0))

        if d.foreign_net is not None:
            ratio = d.foreign_net / base_vol
            vals.append(100 * norm(ratio, -0.02, 0.03))
        if d.institution_net is not None:
            ratio = d.institution_net / base_vol
            vals.append(100 * norm(ratio, -0.02, 0.03))

        # 3일 누적은 당일보다 작은 보조가중
        if d.foreign_3d is not None:
            vals.append(100 * norm(d.foreign_3d / (base_vol * 3), -0.015, 0.025))
        if d.institution_3d is not None:
            vals.append(100 * norm(d.institution_3d / (base_vol * 3), -0.015, 0.025))

        if not vals:
            return 50.0
        return clip100(float(np.mean(vals)))

    @staticmethod
    def program_score(d: DeepMetrics) -> float:
        if d.program_net is None:
            return 50.0
        gross = abs(d.program_buy or 0.0) + abs(d.program_sell or 0.0)
        if gross <= 0:
            # 순매수 부호만 존재하는 경우 중립 근처
            return 60.0 if d.program_net > 0 else 40.0
        ratio = d.program_net / gross
        return clip100(100 * norm(ratio, -0.30, 0.30))

    @staticmethod
    def sector_score(d: DeepMetrics) -> float:
        if d.sector_breadth is None:
            return 50.0
        # breadth 0.35 이하 약함, 0.70 이상 강함
        base = 100 * norm(d.sector_breadth, 0.30, 0.75)
        if d.sector_avg_return is not None:
            base += np.clip(d.sector_avg_return * 4.0, -10, 10)
        return clip100(base)

    def final_score(self, stage1_row: pd.Series, d: DeepMetrics) -> Tuple[float, Dict[str, float], List[str]]:
        s1 = fnum(stage1_row.get("stage1_score"), 0.0)
        ss = self.strength_score(d)
        fs = self.flow_score(d)
        ps = self.program_score(d)
        bs = self.sector_score(d)
        coverage = d.coverage()

        # 구조 45 + 체결강도 15 + 수급 20 + 프로그램 10 + breadth 10
        raw = 0.45 * s1 + 0.15 * ss + 0.20 * fs + 0.10 * ps + 0.10 * bs

        reasons = []
        state = str(stage1_row.get("stage1_state", ""))
        if state == "UP_START":
            raw += 4.0
            reasons.append("1차 상태 UP_START")
        elif state == "REVERSAL_ARMED":
            raw += 3.0
            reasons.append("1차 상태 REVERSAL_ARMED")
        elif state == "UP_EXHAUST":
            raw -= 8.0
            reasons.append("상승 소진 감점")

        # Deep 누락을 0점으로 만들지 않되 신뢰도 감점
        if coverage < 1.0:
            penalty = (1.0 - coverage) * (10.0 if self.fail_open else 25.0)
            raw -= penalty
            reasons.append(f"Deep coverage {coverage:.0%} 감점")

        if d.trade_strength is not None:
            if d.trade_strength >= 140:
                reasons.append(f"체결강도 강함 {d.trade_strength:.1f}")
            elif d.trade_strength < 90:
                reasons.append(f"체결강도 약함 {d.trade_strength:.1f}")

        if d.foreign_net is not None and d.institution_net is not None:
            if d.foreign_net > 0 and d.institution_net > 0:
                reasons.append("외인·기관 동시 순매수")
            elif d.foreign_net < 0 and d.institution_net < 0:
                reasons.append("외인·기관 동시 순매도")

        if d.program_net is not None:
            reasons.append("프로그램 순매수" if d.program_net > 0 else "프로그램 순매도")

        if d.sector_breadth is not None:
            if d.sector_breadth >= 0.65:
                reasons.append("섹터 breadth 확산")
            elif d.sector_breadth < 0.40:
                reasons.append("섹터 breadth 부족")

        return clip100(raw), {
            "stage1_component": round(0.45 * s1, 2),
            "strength_component": round(0.15 * ss, 2),
            "flow_component": round(0.20 * fs, 2),
            "program_component": round(0.10 * ps, 2),
            "sector_component": round(0.10 * bs, 2),
            "deep_coverage": round(coverage, 3),
        }, reasons

    def select_deep_candidates(self, stage1: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
        if stage1.empty:
            return stage1

        # DOWN/ERROR는 원칙적으로 제외.
        # UP_EXHAUST도 신규진입 후보에서는 제외.
        x = stage1[
            stage1["stage1_state"].isin(["UP_START", "REVERSAL_ARMED", "DOWN_WEAKEN", "UP_TREND"])
        ].copy()

        x = x[pd.to_numeric(x["stage1_score"], errors="coerce") >= self.stage1_min_score]

        # UP_START / ARMED 우선, 그 다음 점수
        pri = {"UP_START": 0, "REVERSAL_ARMED": 1, "DOWN_WEAKEN": 2, "UP_TREND": 3}
        x["_p"] = x["stage1_state"].map(pri).fillna(9)
        x = x.sort_values(
            ["_p", "stage1_score", "sector_breadth"],
            ascending=[True, False, False],
            na_position="last",
        ).drop(columns="_p")
        return x.head(self.deep_n).reset_index(drop=True)

    def run_deep(self, stage1_all: pd.DataFrame, candidates: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
        if candidates.empty:
            return candidates

        rows = []
        for i, (_, r) in enumerate(candidates.iterrows(), 1):
            symbol = str(r["symbol"]).zfill(6)
            d = self.deep.collect(symbol)

            # Sector breadth는 1차 전체시장 계산값을 그대로 붙인다.
            d.sector_code = None if pd.isna(r.get("sector_code")) else str(r.get("sector_code"))
            d.sector_breadth = None if pd.isna(r.get("sector_breadth")) else fnum(r.get("sector_breadth"), None)
            d.sector_avg_return = None if pd.isna(r.get("sector_avg_return")) else fnum(r.get("sector_avg_return"), None)
            d.sector_member_count = None if pd.isna(r.get("sector_member_count")) else int(r.get("sector_member_count"))
            d.source_sector = "KIS_MASTER+FULL_UNIVERSE_BREADTH"

            score, comp, reasons = self.final_score(r, d)
            row = dict(r)
            row.update(d.to_dict())
            row.update(comp)
            row["deep_score"] = round(score, 2)
            row["deep_reasons"] = " | ".join(reasons)
            rows.append(row)

            LOG.info(
                "DEEP %d/%d %s %s stage1=%.1f deep=%.1f coverage=%.0f%%",
                i, len(candidates), symbol, r.get("name", ""),
                fnum(r.get("stage1_score")), score, d.coverage() * 100
            )

        out = pd.DataFrame(rows)
        out = out.sort_values(
            ["deep_score", "deep_coverage", "stage1_score"],
            ascending=[False, False, False],
            na_position="last",
        ).reset_index(drop=True)

        # 최종 등급
        def grade(v):
            v = fnum(v)
            if v >= 88: return "S"
            if v >= 80: return "A"
            if v >= 72: return "B"
            if v >= 64: return "WATCH"
            return "REJECT"

        out["deep_grade"] = out["deep_score"].map(grade)
        return out

    def save_results(self, stage1: pd.DataFrame, deep: pd.DataFrame):
        stamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
        p1 = self.result_dir / f"1차전수검색_{stamp}.csv"
        p2 = self.result_dir / f"2차딥스캔_{stamp}.csv"
        p3 = self.result_dir / f"최종변곡후보_{stamp}.csv"

        stage1.to_csv(p1, index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")
        deep.to_csv(p2, index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")

        final = deep[
            (deep["deep_grade"].isin(["S", "A", "B", "WATCH"])) &
            (deep["stage1_state"].isin(["UP_START", "REVERSAL_ARMED", "DOWN_WEAKEN"]))
        ].head(self.final_top_n) if not deep.empty else deep
        final.to_csv(p3, index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")

        summary = {
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
            "stage1_count": int(len(stage1)),
            "deep_count": int(len(deep)),
            "final_count": int(len(final)),
            "stage1_file": str(p1),
            "deep_file": str(p2),
            "final_file": str(p3),
            "top": final[[c for c in [
                "symbol","name","stage1_state","stage1_score","deep_score","deep_grade",
                "trade_strength","foreign_net","institution_net","program_net",
                "sector_breadth","deep_coverage","stage1_price_source",
                "source_strength","source_flow","source_program","source_sector"
            ] if c in final.columns]].to_dict("records") if not final.empty else [],
        }
        (self.result_dir / "latest_deep.json").write_text(
            json.dumps(summary, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default=str),
            encoding="utf-8",
        )
        return p1, p2, p3, final

    def run(
        self,
        universe_csv: Optional[str] = None,
        common_only: bool = True,
        max_symbols: Optional[int] = None,
    ):
        stage1, _ = self.run_stage1(
            universe_csv=universe_csv,
            common_only=common_only,
            max_symbols=max_symbols,
        )
        candidates = self.select_deep_candidates(stage1)
        LOG.info("STAGE1 compressed %d -> DEEP candidates %d", len(stage1), len(candidates))
        deep = self.run_deep(stage1, candidates)
        return (*self.save_results(stage1, deep), stage1, deep)


# ---------------- Self Test ----------------

class MockDeepProvider:
    def collect(self, symbol: str) -> DeepMetrics:
        n = int(symbol[-2:])
        return DeepMetrics(
            symbol=symbol,
            trade_strength=92 + (n % 8) * 9,
            trade_strength_ma5=100,
            trade_strength_ma20=98,
            foreign_net=(-1 if n % 5 == 0 else 1) * (1200 + n * 15),
            institution_net=(-1 if n % 7 == 0 else 1) * (900 + n * 12),
            foreign_3d=2400 + n * 20,
            institution_3d=1800 + n * 17,
            flow_volume_base=250000,
            program_net=(-1 if n % 6 == 0 else 1) * (4000 + n * 30),
            program_buy=15000 + n * 50,
            program_sell=9000 + n * 20,
            source_strength="MOCK",
            source_flow="MOCK",
            source_program="MOCK",
            observed_at=datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
            errors=[],
        )


def self_test():
    rng = np.random.default_rng(42)
    rows = []
    states = ["UP_START", "REVERSAL_ARMED", "DOWN_WEAKEN", "UP_TREND", "UP_EXHAUST", "DOWN"]
    for i in range(90):
        state = states[i % len(states)]
        rows.append({
            "symbol": f"{100000+i:06d}",
            "name": f"TEST{i}",
            "market": "KOSDAQ" if i % 2 else "KOSPI",
            "stage1_state": state,
            "stage1_score": float(np.clip(rng.normal(68, 11), 30, 95)),
            "current_return_pct": float(rng.normal(0.5, 2.0)),
            "sector_code": f"S{i%9}",
            "sector_breadth": float(np.clip(rng.normal(.58, .15), .15, .90)),
            "sector_avg_return": float(rng.normal(.5, 1.2)),
            "sector_member_count": 10,
            "stage1_price_source": "MOCK",
        })
    stage1 = pd.DataFrame(rows)

    obj = object.__new__(TwoStageDeepScanner)
    obj.deep_n = 40
    obj.final_top_n = 20
    obj.stage1_min_score = 48
    obj.sector_min_members = 4
    obj.fail_open = True
    obj.deep = MockDeepProvider()
    obj.result_dir = Path("./results_test")
    obj.result_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

    candidates = obj.select_deep_candidates(stage1)
    deep = obj.run_deep(stage1, candidates)

    assert len(candidates) <= 40
    assert not deep.empty
    assert "deep_score" in deep.columns
    assert "deep_coverage" in deep.columns
    assert deep["deep_score"].between(0, 100).all()

    print("SELF_TEST_PASS")
    print(f"stage1={len(stage1)} deep_candidates={len(candidates)}")
    print(deep[[
        "symbol","stage1_state","stage1_score","deep_score","deep_grade",
        "trade_strength","foreign_net","institution_net","program_net",
        "sector_breadth","deep_coverage"
    ]].head(10).to_string(index=False))


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--universe-csv", default=None)
    ap.add_argument("--include-non-common", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--max-symbols", type=int, default=None, help="API 연결시험용")
    ap.add_argument("--self-test", action="store_true")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    if args.self_test:
        self_test()
        return

    s = TwoStageDeepScanner()
    p1, p2, p3, final, stage1, deep = s.run(
        universe_csv=args.universe_csv,
        common_only=not args.include_non_common,
        max_symbols=args.max_symbols,
    )

    print(f"1차 전체시장: {p1}")
    print(f"2차 Deep: {p2}")
    print(f"최종 후보: {p3}")
    if final.empty:
        print("최종 적격 없음")
    else:
        cols = [c for c in [
            "symbol","name","stage1_state","stage1_score","deep_score","deep_grade",
            "trade_strength","foreign_net","institution_net","program_net",
            "sector_breadth","deep_coverage"
        ] if c in final.columns]
        print(final[cols].to_string(index=False))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
전체시장_알파하이킨아시_KIS_LS_스캐너_V1_0.py

핵심 원칙
---------
- 거래대금/등락률 상위 랭킹을 출발점으로 사용하지 않는다.
- KOSPI + KOSDAQ 전체 유니버스를 종목코드 기준으로 순회한다.
- KRX(J)와 NXT(NX)를 절대 혼합하지 않는다. 본 버전 기본은 KRX(J) 전용이다.
- 한 종목의 한 판정 데이터는 단일 공급자(KIS 또는 LS)로 완결한다.
- KIS 우선 -> 실패 종목만 LS fallback.
- 과거 일봉은 SQLite 캐시에 저장하고 이후 증분 갱신한다.
- 최종 출력은 REVERSAL_ARMED / UP_START를 최우선으로 정렬한다.

환경변수
--------

"""
