def env_float(name: str, default: float) -> float:
    try:
        return float(os.getenv(name, default))
    except Exception:
        return default


def env_int(name: str, default: int) -> int:
    try:
        return int(os.getenv(name, default))
    except Exception:
        return default


@dataclass
class SymbolInfo:
    code: str
    name: str
    market: str
    etf: bool = False
    nxt: bool = False


class RateLimiter:
    def __init__(self, min_interval: float):
        self.min_interval = max(0.0, float(min_interval))
        self.last = 0.0

    def wait(self):
        now = time.monotonic()
        remain = self.min_interval - (now - self.last)
        if remain > 0:
            time.sleep(remain)
        self.last = time.monotonic()


class HistoryStore:
    """종목별 OHLCV 캐시. 공급자/venue를 분리해 데이터 혼합을 방지한다."""

    def __init__(self, db_path: str):
        self.path = Path(db_path)
        self.path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.conn = sqlite3.connect(self.path)
        self.conn.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS bars (
                provider TEXT NOT NULL,
                venue TEXT NOT NULL,
                symbol TEXT NOT NULL,
                timestamp TEXT NOT NULL,
                open REAL,
                high REAL,
                low REAL,
                close REAL,
                volume REAL,
                turnover REAL,
                PRIMARY KEY(provider, venue, symbol, timestamp)
            )
        """)
        self.conn.execute("""
            CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_bars_symbol
            ON bars(provider, venue, symbol, timestamp)
        """)
        self.conn.commit()

    def upsert(self, provider: str, venue: str, symbol: str, df: pd.DataFrame):
        if df is None or df.empty:
            return
        x = df.copy()
        for c in ["open", "high", "low", "close", "volume"]:
            if c not in x.columns:
                raise ValueError(f"cache upsert missing {c}")
        if "turnover" not in x.columns:
            x["turnover"] = np.nan

        rows = []
        for _, r in x.iterrows():
            ts = pd.Timestamp(r["timestamp"]).strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
            rows.append((
                provider, venue, symbol, ts,
                float(r["open"]), float(r["high"]), float(r["low"]),
                float(r["close"]), float(r["volume"]),
                None if pd.isna(r["turnover"]) else float(r["turnover"]),
            ))
        self.conn.executemany("""
            INSERT INTO bars(provider, venue, symbol, timestamp, open, high, low, close, volume, turnover)
            VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)
            ON CONFLICT(provider, venue, symbol, timestamp) DO UPDATE SET
                open=excluded.open, high=excluded.high, low=excluded.low,
                close=excluded.close, volume=excluded.volume, turnover=excluded.turnover
        """, rows)
        self.conn.commit()

    def read(self, provider: str, venue: str, symbol: str, limit: int = 240) -> pd.DataFrame:
        q = """
            SELECT timestamp, open, high, low, close, volume, turnover
            FROM bars
            WHERE provider=? AND venue=? AND symbol=?
            ORDER BY timestamp DESC LIMIT ?
        """
        df = pd.read_sql_query(q, self.conn, params=(provider, venue, symbol, limit))
        if df.empty:
            return df
        df["timestamp"] = pd.to_datetime(df["timestamp"])
        return df.sort_values("timestamp").reset_index(drop=True)

    def last_date(self, provider: str, venue: str, symbol: str) -> Optional[date]:
        cur = self.conn.execute("""
            SELECT MAX(timestamp) FROM bars
            WHERE provider=? AND venue=? AND symbol=?
        """, (provider, venue, symbol))
        row = cur.fetchone()
        if not row or not row[0]:
            return None
        return pd.Timestamp(row[0]).date()


class BrokerError(RuntimeError):
    pass


class BaseProvider:
    name = "BASE"

    def get_universe(self) -> List[SymbolInfo]:
        raise NotImplementedError

    def get_daily_bars(self, symbol: str, start: date, end: date) -> pd.DataFrame:
        raise NotImplementedError


class KISProvider(BaseProvider):
    name = "KIS"

    def __init__(self):
        self.app_key = os.getenv("KIS_APP_KEY", "").strip()
        self.app_secret = os.getenv("KIS_APP_SECRET", "").strip()
        self.base = os.getenv("KIS_BASE_URL", "https://openapi.koreainvestment.com:9443").rstrip("/")
        self.timeout = env_float("REQUEST_TIMEOUT", 12.0)
        self.rl = RateLimiter(env_float("KIS_MIN_INTERVAL", 0.08))
        self.s = requests.Session()
        self.token: Optional[str] = None
        self.token_expire_monotonic = 0.0

    @property
    def configured(self) -> bool:
        return bool(self.app_key and self.app_secret)

    def _ensure_token(self):
        if self.token and time.monotonic() < self.token_expire_monotonic - 60:
            return
        if not self.configured:
            raise BrokerError("KIS_APP_KEY/KIS_APP_SECRET 미설정")
        self.rl.wait()
        r = self.s.post(
            self.base + "/oauth2/tokenP",
            json={
                "grant_type": "client_credentials",
                "appkey": self.app_key,
                "appsecret": self.app_secret,
            },
            timeout=self.timeout,
        )
        if r.status_code != 200:
            raise BrokerError(f"KIS token HTTP {r.status_code}: {r.text[:200]}")
        j = r.json()
        self.token = j.get("access_token")
        if not self.token:
            raise BrokerError(f"KIS token missing: {j}")
        # 공식 유효기간과 무관하게 프로세스 내에서 보수적으로 캐시
        self.token_expire_monotonic = time.monotonic() + 6 * 3600

    def _headers(self, tr_id: str) -> Dict[str, str]:
        self._ensure_token()
        return {
            "authorization": f"Bearer {self.token}",
            "appkey": self.app_key,
            "appsecret": self.app_secret,
            "tr_id": tr_id,
            "content-type": "application/json; charset=utf-8",
            "custtype": "P",
        }

    @staticmethod
    def _parse_master_bytes(content: bytes, market: str) -> List[SymbolInfo]:
        items: List[SymbolInfo] = []
        for line in content.split(b"\n"):
            if len(line) < 61:
                continue
            code = line[0:9].decode("euc-kr", errors="ignore").strip()
            if len(code) > 6:
                code = code[-6:]
            name = line[21:61].decode("euc-kr", errors="ignore").strip()
            if re.fullmatch(r"\d{6}", code or "") and name:
                items.append(SymbolInfo(code=code, name=name, market=market))
        return items

    def get_universe(self) -> List[SymbolInfo]:
        items: List[SymbolInfo] = []
        for market, url in KIS_MASTER_URLS.items():
            self.rl.wait()
            r = self.s.get(url, timeout=max(self.timeout, 30))
            r.raise_for_status()
            raw = r.content
            try:
                with zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(raw)) as zf:
                    first = zf.namelist()[0]
                    raw = zf.read(first)
            except zipfile.BadZipFile:
                pass
            items.extend(self._parse_master_bytes(raw, market))
        return items

    def get_daily_bars(self, symbol: str, start: date, end: date) -> pd.DataFrame:
        self.rl.wait()
        params = {
            "FID_COND_MRKT_DIV_CODE": "J",  # KRX. NX/UN과 절대 혼합 금지.
            "FID_INPUT_ISCD": symbol,
            "FID_INPUT_DATE_1": start.strftime("%Y%m%d"),
            "FID_INPUT_DATE_2": end.strftime("%Y%m%d"),
            "FID_PERIOD_DIV_CODE": "D",
            "FID_ORG_ADJ_PRC": "0",
        }
        r = self.s.get(
            self.base + "/uapi/domestic-stock/v1/quotations/inquire-daily-itemchartprice",
            headers=self._headers("FHKST03010100"),
            params=params,
            timeout=self.timeout,
        )
        if r.status_code != 200:
            raise BrokerError(f"KIS {symbol} HTTP {r.status_code}: {r.text[:180]}")
        j = r.json()
        rows = j.get("output2") or []
        if not rows:
            raise BrokerError(f"KIS {symbol} output2 empty: {j.get('msg1', '')}")

        out = []
        for z in rows:
            try:
                out.append({
                    "timestamp": pd.to_datetime(str(z.get("stck_bsop_date")), format="%Y%m%d"),
                    "open": float(z.get("stck_oprc", 0)),
                    "high": float(z.get("stck_hgpr", 0)),
                    "low": float(z.get("stck_lwpr", 0)),
                    "close": float(z.get("stck_clpr", 0)),
                    "volume": float(z.get("acml_vol", 0)),
                    "turnover": float(z.get("acml_tr_pbmn", 0) or 0),
                })
            except Exception:
                continue
        df = pd.DataFrame(out)
        if df.empty:
            raise BrokerError(f"KIS {symbol} parsing empty")
        return df.sort_values("timestamp").drop_duplicates("timestamp").reset_index(drop=True)


class LSProvider(BaseProvider):
    name = "LS"

    def __init__(self):
        self.app_key = os.getenv("LS_APP_KEY", "").strip()
        self.app_secret = os.getenv("LS_APP_SECRET", "").strip()
        self.base = os.getenv("LS_BASE_URL", "https://openapi.ls-sec.co.kr:8080").rstrip("/")
        self.chart_path = os.getenv("LS_CHART_PATH", "/stock/chart")
        self.market_path = os.getenv("LS_MARKET_DATA_PATH", "/stock/market-data")
        self.mac = os.getenv("LS_MAC_ADDRESS", "").strip()
        self.timeout = env_float("REQUEST_TIMEOUT", 12.0)
        self.rl = RateLimiter(env_float("LS_MIN_INTERVAL", 1.05))
        self.s = requests.Session()
        self.token: Optional[str] = None
        self.token_expire_monotonic = 0.0

    @property
    def configured(self) -> bool:
        return bool(self.app_key and self.app_secret)

    def _ensure_token(self):
        if self.token and time.monotonic() < self.token_expire_monotonic - 60:
            return
        if not self.configured:
            raise BrokerError("LS_APP_KEY/LS_APP_SECRET 미설정")
        self.rl.wait()
        r = self.s.post(
            self.base + "/oauth2/token",
            headers={"content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded"},
            data={
                "grant_type": "client_credentials",
                "appkey": self.app_key,
                "appsecretkey": self.app_secret,
                "scope": "oob",
            },
            timeout=self.timeout,
        )
        if r.status_code != 200:
            raise BrokerError(f"LS token HTTP {r.status_code}: {r.text[:200]}")
        j = r.json()
        self.token = j.get("access_token")
        if not self.token:
            raise BrokerError(f"LS token missing: {j}")
        expires = int(j.get("expires_in") or j.get("expire_in") or 86400)
        self.token_expire_monotonic = time.monotonic() + max(600, expires)

    def _headers(self, tr_cd: str, tr_cont: str = "N", tr_cont_key: str = "") -> Dict[str, str]:
        self._ensure_token()
        h = {
            "content-type": "application/json; charset=UTF-8",
            "authorization": f"Bearer {self.token}",
            "tr_cd": tr_cd,
            "tr_cont": tr_cont,
            "tr_cont_key": tr_cont_key,
        }
        if self.mac:
            h["mac_address"] = self.mac
        return h

    def _post_tr(self, path: str, tr_cd: str, body: dict) -> requests.Response:
        self.rl.wait()
        r = self.s.post(
            self.base + path,
            headers=self._headers(tr_cd),
            json=body,
            timeout=self.timeout,
        )
        if r.status_code != 200:
            raise BrokerError(f"LS {tr_cd} HTTP {r.status_code}: {r.text[:200]}")
        return r

    def get_universe(self) -> List[SymbolInfo]:
        items: List[SymbolInfo] = []
        for market, gubun in [("KOSPI", "1"), ("KOSDAQ", "2")]:
            r = self._post_tr(
                self.market_path,
                "t9945",
                {"t9945InBlock": {"gubun": gubun}},
            )
            j = r.json()
            rows = j.get("t9945OutBlock") or []
            for z in rows:
                code = str(z.get("shcode", "")).strip()
                name = str(z.get("hname", "")).strip()
                if re.fullmatch(r"\d{6}", code) and name:
                    items.append(SymbolInfo(
                        code=code,
                        name=name,
                        market=market,
                        etf=str(z.get("etfchk", "0")).strip() not in ("", "0", "N"),
                        nxt=str(z.get("nxt_chk", "0")).strip() not in ("", "0", "N"),
                    ))
        if not items:
            raise BrokerError("LS t9945 universe empty")
        return items

    def get_daily_bars(self, symbol: str, start: date, end: date) -> pd.DataFrame:
        # t8410: KRX 주식 일/주/월/년 차트. 통합(NXT 포함) 차트는 사용하지 않음.
        body = {
            "t8410InBlock": {
                "shcode": symbol,
                "gubun": "2",
                "qrycnt": 500,
                "sdate": start.strftime("%Y%m%d"),
                "edate": end.strftime("%Y%m%d"),
                "cts_date": "",
                "comp_yn": "N",
                "sujung": "Y",
            }
        }
        r = self._post_tr(self.chart_path, "t8410", body)
        j = r.json()
        rows = j.get("t8410OutBlock1") or []
        if not rows:
            raise BrokerError(f"LS {symbol} t8410 empty")

        out = []
        for z in rows:
            try:
                out.append({
                    "timestamp": pd.to_datetime(str(z.get("date")), format="%Y%m%d"),
                    "open": float(z.get("open", 0)),
                    "high": float(z.get("high", 0)),
                    "low": float(z.get("low", 0)),
                    "close": float(z.get("close", 0)),
                    "volume": float(z.get("jdiff_vol", 0)),
                    "turnover": float(z.get("value", 0) or 0),
                })
            except Exception:
                continue
        df = pd.DataFrame(out)
        if df.empty:
            raise BrokerError(f"LS {symbol} parsing empty")
        return df.sort_values("timestamp").drop_duplicates("timestamp").reset_index(drop=True)


def is_probably_common_stock(item: SymbolInfo) -> bool:
    """마스터 메타데이터가 제한적인 경우의 보수적 휴리스틱."""
    name = item.name.upper().replace(" ", "")
    if item.etf:
        return False

    deny_tokens = [
        "KODEX", "TIGER", "ACE", "KBSTAR", "RISE", "SOL", "HANARO",
        "KOSEF", "PLUS", "TIMEFOLIO", "ARIRANG", "ETN", "스팩", "SPAC", "리츠",
    ]
    if any(t.upper() in name for t in deny_tokens):
        return False

    # 명백한 우선주 표기만 제외. 일반 종목명의 '우' 글자는 건드리지 않는다.
    if re.search(r"(우|우B|우C|1우|2우B|3우C)$", item.name):
        return False
    return True


def dedupe_universe(items: Iterable[SymbolInfo], common_only: bool = True) -> List[SymbolInfo]:
    d: Dict[str, SymbolInfo] = {}
    for x in items:
        if not re.fullmatch(r"\d{6}", x.code):
            continue
        if common_only and not is_probably_common_stock(x):
            continue
        d[x.code] = x
    return sorted(d.values(), key=lambda z: (z.market, z.code))


def load_universe_csv(path: str) -> List[SymbolInfo]:
    df = pd.read_csv(path, dtype=str)
    code_col = next((c for c in ["code", "symbol", "종목코드"] if c in df.columns), None)
    name_col = next((c for c in ["name", "종목명", "한글명"] if c in df.columns), None)
    market_col = next((c for c in ["market", "시장"] if c in df.columns), None)
    if not code_col:
        raise ValueError("유니버스 CSV에 code 또는 symbol 또는 종목코드 컬럼 필요")
    items = []
    for _, r in df.iterrows():
        code = str(r[code_col]).zfill(6)
        items.append(SymbolInfo(
            code=code,
            name=str(r[name_col]) if name_col else code,
            market=str(r[market_col]) if market_col else "KRX",
        ))
    return items


class MarketScanner:
    def __init__(self):
        if os.getenv("VENUE", "KRX").upper() != "KRX":
            raise ValueError("V1.0은 DATA TRUTH GATE를 위해 KRX 전용입니다. NXT/UN 혼합을 허용하지 않습니다.")

        self.kis = KISProvider()
        self.ls = LSProvider()
        self.provider_mode = os.getenv("SCAN_PROVIDER", "auto").lower()
        self.lookback_days = env_int("LOOKBACK_DAYS", 180)
        self.min_bars = env_int("MIN_BARS", 70)
        self.top_n = env_int("TOP_N", 20)
        self.store = HistoryStore(os.getenv("CACHE_DB", "./data/alpha_ha_cache.sqlite3"))
        self.result_dir = Path(os.getenv("RESULT_DIR", "./results"))
        self.result_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.engine = AlphaHeikinAshiEngine(EngineConfig())

    def providers(self) -> List[BaseProvider]:
        if self.provider_mode == "kis":
            return [self.kis]
        if self.provider_mode == "ls":
            return [self.ls]
        # auto: KIS -> LS
        return [self.kis, self.ls]

    def get_universe(self, csv_path: Optional[str] = None, common_only: bool = True) -> List[SymbolInfo]:
        if csv_path:
            return dedupe_universe(load_universe_csv(csv_path), common_only)

        # 유니버스 자체는 가능한 공급자에서 한 번만 받는다.
        # KIS/LS 가격 우선순위와 독립적이다.
        errs = []
        for p in self.providers():
            try:
                if getattr(p, "configured", True) or isinstance(p, KISProvider):
                    u = p.get_universe()
                    if u:
                        LOG.info("universe source=%s count=%d", p.name, len(u))
                        return dedupe_universe(u, common_only)
            except Exception as e:
                errs.append(f"{p.name}:{e}")
        raise BrokerError("유니버스 수집 실패: " + " | ".join(errs))

    def _fetch_one_provider(self, p: BaseProvider, symbol: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
        provider = p.name
        cached = self.store.read(provider, "KRX", symbol, limit=max(240, self.min_bars + 20))
        last = self.store.last_date(provider, "KRX", symbol)

        today = date.today()
        if last is None or len(cached) < self.min_bars:
            start = today - timedelta(days=self.lookback_days)
        else:
            # 마지막 봉을 다시 요청해 당일 미완성 봉/정정값을 갱신
            start = max(today - timedelta(days=10), last - timedelta(days=3))

        fresh = p.get_daily_bars(symbol, start, today)
        self.store.upsert(provider, "KRX", symbol, fresh)
        merged = self.store.read(provider, "KRX", symbol, limit=max(240, self.min_bars + 20))
        if len(merged) < self.min_bars:
            raise BrokerError(f"{provider} {symbol}: bars {len(merged)} < MIN_BARS {self.min_bars}")
        return merged

    def get_symbol_data(self, symbol: str) -> Tuple[pd.DataFrame, str, List[str]]:
        errs = []
        for p in self.providers():
            if hasattr(p, "configured") and not p.configured:
                errs.append(f"{p.name}:NOT_CONFIGURED")
                continue
            try:
                df = self._fetch_one_provider(p, symbol)
                return df, p.name, errs
            except Exception as e:
                errs.append(f"{p.name}:{type(e).__name__}:{str(e)[:120]}")
        raise BrokerError(" | ".join(errs))

    @staticmethod
    def priority(state: str) -> int:
        order = {
            "UP_START": 0,
            "REVERSAL_ARMED": 1,
            "UP_TREND": 2,
            "DOWN_WEAKEN": 3,
            "UP_EXHAUST": 4,
            "DOWN": 5,
            "ERROR": 9,
        }
        return order.get(state, 8)

    def run(
        self,
        universe_csv: Optional[str] = None,
        common_only: bool = True,
        max_symbols: Optional[int] = None,
    ) -> pd.DataFrame:
        universe = self.get_universe(universe_csv, common_only=common_only)
        if max_symbols:
            universe = universe[:max_symbols]

        LOG.info("ZERO-BASE full universe scan start: %d symbols", len(universe))
        rows = []
        started = time.time()

        for i, item in enumerate(universe, 1):
            try:
                df, provider, prior_errors = self.get_symbol_data(item.code)
                result = self.engine.analyze(df, symbol=item.code)
                d = result.to_dict()
                d.update({
                    "name": item.name,
                    "market": item.market,
                    "provider": provider,
                    "venue": "KRX",
                    "fallback_trace": " | ".join(prior_errors),
                })
                rows.append(d)
            except Exception as e:
                rows.append({
                    "symbol": item.code,
                    "name": item.name,
                    "market": item.market,
                    "provider": "",
                    "venue": "KRX",
                    "state": "ERROR",
                    "score": 0.0,
                    "grade": "ERROR",
                    "error": str(e)[:300],
                })

            if i % 50 == 0 or i == len(universe):
                LOG.info("progress %d/%d elapsed=%.1fs", i, len(universe), time.time() - started)

        out = pd.DataFrame(rows)
        if out.empty:
            return out

        out["_priority"] = out["state"].map(self.priority).fillna(8)
        out = out.sort_values(
            ["_priority", "score", "p_up_5d"],
            ascending=[True, False, False],
            na_position="last",
        ).drop(columns=["_priority"]).reset_index(drop=True)

        stamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
        all_path = self.result_dir / f"전체시장판정_{stamp}.csv"
        out.to_csv(all_path, index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")

        candidates = out[
            (out["state"].isin(["REVERSAL_ARMED", "UP_START"])) &
            (pd.to_numeric(out["score"], errors="coerce") >= 60)
        ].copy()

        cand_path = self.result_dir / f"변곡후보_{stamp}.csv"
        candidates.head(self.top_n).to_csv(cand_path, index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")

        summary = {
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
            "universe_count": len(universe),
            "success_count": int((out["state"] != "ERROR").sum()),
            "error_count": int((out["state"] == "ERROR").sum()),
            "armed_count": int((out["state"] == "REVERSAL_ARMED").sum()),
            "up_start_count": int((out["state"] == "UP_START").sum()),
            "top_candidates": candidates.head(self.top_n)[
                [c for c in ["symbol", "name", "state", "score", "grade",
                             "p_up_1d", "p_up_3d", "p_up_5d", "provider", "venue"]
                 if c in candidates.columns]
            ].to_dict("records"),
            "all_result": str(all_path),
            "candidate_result": str(cand_path),
        }
        (self.result_dir / "latest.json").write_text(
            json.dumps(summary, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default=str),
            encoding="utf-8",
        )
        return out


def build_mock_universe(n=40, bars=110):
    """네트워크 없이 파이프라인 구조를 검증하는 테스트 데이터."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(17)
    universe = {}
    for i in range(n):
        code = f"{100000+i:06d}"
        drift = rng.normal(0.02, 0.03)
        close = 100 + np.cumsum(rng.normal(drift, 1.0, bars))
        if i % 7 == 0:
            close[-15:] += np.linspace(-3, 7, 15)
        op = np.r_[close[0], close[:-1]] + rng.normal(0, .25, bars)
        hi = np.maximum(op, close) + rng.uniform(.1, .9, bars)
        lo = np.minimum(op, close) - rng.uniform(.1, .9, bars)
        vol = rng.lognormal(12, .35, bars)
        if i % 7 == 0:
            vol[-5:] *= np.linspace(1.0, 1.7, 5)
        universe[code] = pd.DataFrame({
            "timestamp": pd.date_range("2026-01-01", periods=bars, freq="B"),
            "open": op, "high": hi, "low": lo, "close": close, "volume": vol,
            "market_return": rng.normal(0, .006, bars),
            "sector_return": rng.normal(0, .008, bars),
        })
    return universe


def self_test():
    engine = AlphaHeikinAshiEngine()
    data = build_mock_universe()
    result = engine.scan_universe(
        data,
        top_n=10,
        allowed_states=[TrendState.REVERSAL_ARMED, TrendState.UP_START, TrendState.UP_TREND],
    )
    print("SELF_TEST_PASS")
    if not result.empty:
        cols = [c for c in ["symbol", "state", "score", "grade", "p_up_5d"] if c in result.columns]
        print(result[cols].head(10).to_string(index=False))


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--universe-csv", default=None)
    ap.add_argument("--include-non-common", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--max-symbols", type=int, default=None, help="연결시험용. 실전 전체시장에서는 미사용")
    ap.add_argument("--self-test", action="store_true")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    if args.self_test:
        self_test()
        return

    scanner = MarketScanner()
    result = scanner.run(
        universe_csv=args.universe_csv,
        common_only=not args.include_non_common,
        max_symbols=args.max_symbols,
    )
    if result.empty:
        print("적격 없음 / 결과 없음")
        return

    view = result[result["state"].isin(["UP_START", "REVERSAL_ARMED"])].head(scanner.top_n)
    if view.empty:
        print("확정 변곡후보 없음")
        near = result[result["state"].isin(["UP_TREND", "DOWN_WEAKEN"])].head(5)
        if not near.empty:
            print("\nNEAR:")
            print(near[[c for c in ["symbol", "name", "state", "score", "provider"] if c in near.columns]].to_string(index=False))
    else:
        print(view[[c for c in [
            "symbol", "name", "state", "score", "grade",
            "p_up_1d", "p_up_3d", "p_up_5d",
            "rvol", "rs_market", "rs_sector", "provider", "venue"
        ] if c in view.columns]].to_string(index=False))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# embedded: 알파하이킨아시_변곡예측엔진_V1_0.py

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
알파하이킨아시_변곡예측엔진_V1_0.py

목적
----
1) 일반 Heikin-Ashi(Fast HA)로 변곡을 빠르게 감지
2) Alpha Heikin-Ashi(이중 평활)로 노이즈/가짜 반전을 줄임
3) 실제 가격(VWAP/EMA/ATR), 모멘텀(RSI), 거래량(RVOL),
   상대강도(RS), 수급(외국인/기관/프로그램)을 결합
4) DOWN -> DOWN_WEAKEN -> REVERSAL_ARMED -> UP_START ->
   UP_TREND -> UP_EXHAUST 상태를 판정
5) 단일 종목 및 전체 유니버스 스캔 지원
6) 과거 데이터가 있으면 1/3/5 거래일 상승확률을 경험적으로 보정

주의
----
- HA 가격은 실제 체결가격이 아니라 '특징값(feature)'으로만 사용한다.
- 백테스트/매매 체결가는 실제 OHLC/체결가를 사용한다.
- probability_calibrated=False일 때의 확률은 규칙 기반 추정치이며,
  실전 확률로 사용하려면 calibrate_probabilities()로 보정한다.

필수 컬럼
---------
timestamp, open, high, low, close, volume

선택 컬럼
---------
vwap, foreign_net, institution_net, program_net,
market_return, sector_return, turnover

권장
----
- 일봉/60분/15분 데이터를 각각 분석한 뒤 MultiTimeframeEngine으로 결합
- 한국주식 전체시장 스캔 시 데이터 수집부(KIS/LS/키움)는 별도 모듈로 유지
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
from enum import Enum
from typing import Dict, Iterable, List, Mapping, Optional, Tuple, Any
import math
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd


class TrendState(str, Enum):
    DOWN = "DOWN"
    DOWN_WEAKEN = "DOWN_WEAKEN"
    REVERSAL_ARMED = "REVERSAL_ARMED"
    UP_START = "UP_START"
    UP_TREND = "UP_TREND"
    UP_EXHAUST = "UP_EXHAUST"


@dataclass
class EngineConfig:
    # 평활
    alpha_pre_ema: int = 3
    alpha_post_ema: int = 3

    # 지표
    atr_period: int = 14
    rsi_period: int = 14
    ema_fast: int = 10
    ema_mid: int = 20
    ema_slow: int = 60
    rvol_period: int = 20
    rs_period: int = 5

    # 변곡/추세 판정
    doji_body_atr_max: float = 0.18
    strong_body_atr_min: float = 0.45
    weak_body_atr_max: float = 0.28
    wick_ratio_trigger: float = 0.35
    compression_lookback: int = 4
    slope_lookback: int = 3

    # 거래량/실전 게이트
    rvol_min: float = 1.10
    rvol_strong: float = 1.35
    vwap_tolerance_atr: float = 0.35
    price_extension_atr: float = 2.0

    # 점수 가중치 (합계 100)
    w_ha_structure: float = 20.0
    w_reversal: float = 20.0
    w_price_hold: float = 15.0
    w_rs: float = 15.0
    w_volume: float = 10.0
    w_flow: float = 10.0
    w_sector: float = 10.0

    # 등급
    score_armed: float = 70.0
    score_strong: float = 80.0
    score_top: float = 88.0

    # 선행 포착 선호
    max_preferred_daily_gain_pct: float = 3.5


@dataclass
class SignalResult:
    symbol: str
    timestamp: Any
    state: str
    score: float
    grade: str

    fast_ha_up: bool
    alpha_ha_up: bool
    fast_alpha_divergence: str

    body_atr: float
    body_compression: float
    lower_wick_ratio: float
    upper_wick_ratio: float

    close: float
    vwap: Optional[float]
    above_vwap: Optional[bool]
    ema20_hold: bool
    rsi: float
    rvol: float

    rs_market: float
    rs_sector: float

    flow_score: float
    sector_score: float

    p_up_1d: float
    p_up_3d: float
    p_up_5d: float
    probability_calibrated: bool

    reasons: List[str]
    warnings: List[str]

    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return asdict(self)


def _safe_div(a, b, default=0.0):
    if b is None:
        return default
    try:
        if abs(float(b)) < 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(float(b)):
            return default
        v = float(a) / float(b)
        return v if np.isfinite(v) else default
    except Exception:
        return default


def _sigmoid(x: float) -> float:
    return 1.0 / (1.0 + math.exp(-max(-20.0, min(20.0, x))))


def _normalize_0_1(x: float, lo: float, hi: float) -> float:
    if hi <= lo:
        return 0.0
    return float(np.clip((x - lo) / (hi - lo), 0.0, 1.0))


class AlphaHeikinAshiEngine:
    def __init__(self, config: Optional[EngineConfig] = None):
        self.cfg = config or EngineConfig()
        self.calibration: Dict[str, Dict[str, Dict[int, float]]] = {}

    # ---------- 기본 지표 ----------
    @staticmethod
    def _validate(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
        required = ["open", "high", "low", "close", "volume"]
        missing = [c for c in required if c not in df.columns]
        if missing:
            raise ValueError(f"필수 컬럼 누락: {missing}")

        x = df.copy()
        if "timestamp" not in x.columns:
            x["timestamp"] = np.arange(len(x))

        x = x.sort_values("timestamp").reset_index(drop=True)

        for c in required:
            x[c] = pd.to_numeric(x[c], errors="coerce")

        x = x.dropna(subset=required).reset_index(drop=True)
        if len(x) < 10:
            raise ValueError("최소 10개 이상의 봉이 필요합니다.")
        return x

    @staticmethod
    def _ema(s: pd.Series, span: int) -> pd.Series:
        return s.ewm(span=span, adjust=False, min_periods=1).mean()

    def _atr(self, df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.Series:
        prev_close = df["close"].shift(1)
        tr = pd.concat([
            df["high"] - df["low"],
            (df["high"] - prev_close).abs(),
            (df["low"] - prev_close).abs(),
        ], axis=1).max(axis=1)
        return tr.ewm(alpha=1.0 / self.cfg.atr_period, adjust=False, min_periods=1).mean()

    def _rsi(self, close: pd.Series) -> pd.Series:
        delta = close.diff()
        gain = delta.clip(lower=0)
        loss = -delta.clip(upper=0)
        avg_gain = gain.ewm(alpha=1.0 / self.cfg.rsi_period, adjust=False, min_periods=1).mean()
        avg_loss = loss.ewm(alpha=1.0 / self.cfg.rsi_period, adjust=False, min_periods=1).mean()
        rs = avg_gain / avg_loss.replace(0, np.nan)
        rsi = 100 - (100 / (1 + rs))
        return rsi.fillna(50.0)

    @staticmethod
    def _heikin_ashi_from_ohlc(o, h, l, c) -> pd.DataFrame:
        ha_close = (o + h + l + c) / 4.0
        ha_open = np.zeros(len(ha_close), dtype=float)
        ha_open[0] = (float(o.iloc[0]) + float(c.iloc[0])) / 2.0

        for i in range(1, len(ha_close)):
            ha_open[i] = (ha_open[i - 1] + float(ha_close.iloc[i - 1])) / 2.0

        ha_open = pd.Series(ha_open, index=o.index)
        ha_high = pd.concat([h, ha_open, ha_close], axis=1).max(axis=1)
        ha_low = pd.concat([l, ha_open, ha_close], axis=1).min(axis=1)

        return pd.DataFrame({
            "ha_open": ha_open,
            "ha_high": ha_high,
            "ha_low": ha_low,
            "ha_close": ha_close,
        })

    def _build_fast_and_alpha_ha(self, df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
        # Fast HA
        fast = self._heikin_ashi_from_ohlc(
            df["open"], df["high"], df["low"], df["close"]
        ).add_prefix("fast_")

        # Alpha HA: 실제 OHLC 1차 EMA 평활 -> HA -> HA 자체 2차 EMA 평활
        pre = pd.DataFrame({
            "open": self._ema(df["open"], self.cfg.alpha_pre_ema),
            "high": self._ema(df["high"], self.cfg.alpha_pre_ema),
            "low": self._ema(df["low"], self.cfg.alpha_pre_ema),
            "close": self._ema(df["close"], self.cfg.alpha_pre_ema),
        })
        alpha_raw = self._heikin_ashi_from_ohlc(
            pre["open"], pre["high"], pre["low"], pre["close"]
        )

        alpha = pd.DataFrame({
            "alpha_ha_open": self._ema(alpha_raw["ha_open"], self.cfg.alpha_post_ema),
            "alpha_ha_high": self._ema(alpha_raw["ha_high"], self.cfg.alpha_post_ema),
            "alpha_ha_low": self._ema(alpha_raw["ha_low"], self.cfg.alpha_post_ema),
            "alpha_ha_close": self._ema(alpha_raw["ha_close"], self.cfg.alpha_post_ema),
        })

        return pd.concat([fast, alpha], axis=1)

    def enrich(self, df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
        x = self._validate(df)
        ha = self._build_fast_and_alpha_ha(x)
        x = pd.concat([x, ha], axis=1)

        x["atr"] = self._atr(x)
        x["rsi"] = self._rsi(x["close"])
        x["ema10"] = self._ema(x["close"], self.cfg.ema_fast)
        x["ema20"] = self._ema(x["close"], self.cfg.ema_mid)
        x["ema60"] = self._ema(x["close"], self.cfg.ema_slow)

        vol_ma = x["volume"].rolling(self.cfg.rvol_period, min_periods=3).mean()
        x["rvol"] = (x["volume"] / vol_ma.replace(0, np.nan)).fillna(1.0)

        # VWAP 미제공 시 전형가격 기반 rolling proxy
        if "vwap" not in x.columns:
            typical = (x["high"] + x["low"] + x["close"]) / 3.0
            pv = typical * x["volume"]
            win = min(20, max(5, self.cfg.rvol_period))
            x["vwap"] = (
                pv.rolling(win, min_periods=1).sum()
                / x["volume"].rolling(win, min_periods=1).sum().replace(0, np.nan)
            ).fillna(x["close"])

        # HA 구조
        x["fast_up"] = x["fast_ha_close"] > x["fast_ha_open"]
        x["alpha_up"] = x["alpha_ha_close"] > x["alpha_ha_open"]

        x["fast_body"] = (x["fast_ha_close"] - x["fast_ha_open"]).abs()
        x["body_atr"] = x["fast_body"] / x["atr"].replace(0, np.nan)

        upper = x["fast_ha_high"] - x[["fast_ha_open", "fast_ha_close"]].max(axis=1)
        lower = x[["fast_ha_open", "fast_ha_close"]].min(axis=1) - x["fast_ha_low"]
        full = (x["fast_ha_high"] - x["fast_ha_low"]).replace(0, np.nan)
        x["upper_wick_ratio"] = (upper / full).fillna(0.0)
        x["lower_wick_ratio"] = (lower / full).fillna(0.0)

        look = self.cfg.compression_lookback
        recent_body_mean = x["body_atr"].rolling(look, min_periods=2).mean()
        prev_body_mean = x["body_atr"].shift(look).rolling(look, min_periods=2).mean()
        x["body_compression"] = (1.0 - (recent_body_mean / prev_body_mean.replace(0, np.nan))).clip(-1, 1).fillna(0.0)

        # Alpha 기울기
        sl = self.cfg.slope_lookback
        x["alpha_slope"] = x["alpha_ha_close"] - x["alpha_ha_close"].shift(sl)
        x["fast_slope"] = x["fast_ha_close"] - x["fast_ha_close"].shift(sl)

        # 상대강도: market_return/sector_return이 있으면 누적 차이 사용
        stock_ret = x["close"].pct_change()
        if "market_return" in x.columns:
            market_ret = pd.to_numeric(x["market_return"], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)
        else:
            market_ret = pd.Series(0.0, index=x.index)

        if "sector_return" in x.columns:
            sector_ret = pd.to_numeric(x["sector_return"], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)
        else:
            sector_ret = pd.Series(0.0, index=x.index)

        rp = self.cfg.rs_period
        x["rs_market"] = (
            (1 + stock_ret.fillna(0)).rolling(rp, min_periods=1).apply(np.prod, raw=True) - 1
        ) - (
            (1 + market_ret).rolling(rp, min_periods=1).apply(np.prod, raw=True) - 1
        )
        x["rs_sector"] = (
            (1 + stock_ret.fillna(0)).rolling(rp, min_periods=1).apply(np.prod, raw=True) - 1
        ) - (
            (1 + sector_ret).rolling(rp, min_periods=1).apply(np.prod, raw=True) - 1
        )

        # 수급 합성
        flow_cols = [c for c in ["foreign_net", "institution_net", "program_net"] if c in x.columns]
        if flow_cols:
            # 규모차이를 줄이기 위한 rolling z-score
            zs = []
            for c in flow_cols:
                s = pd.to_numeric(x[c], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)
                mu = s.rolling(20, min_periods=5).mean()
                sd = s.rolling(20, min_periods=5).std().replace(0, np.nan)
                z = ((s - mu) / sd).clip(-3, 3).fillna(0.0)
                zs.append(z)
            x["flow_z"] = pd.concat(zs, axis=1).mean(axis=1)
        else:
            x["flow_z"] = 0.0

        return x

    # ---------- 상태 판정 ----------
    def _state(self, x: pd.DataFrame) -> TrendState:
        r = x.iloc[-1]
        p = x.iloc[-2]

        fast_up = bool(r["fast_up"])
        alpha_up = bool(r["alpha_up"])
        body = float(r["body_atr"])
        comp = float(r["body_compression"])
        low_w = float(r["lower_wick_ratio"])
        up_w = float(r["upper_wick_ratio"])
        alpha_slope = float(r["alpha_slope"]) if np.isfinite(r["alpha_slope"]) else 0.0
        fast_slope = float(r["fast_slope"]) if np.isfinite(r["fast_slope"]) else 0.0

        # 상승 지속
        if fast_up and alpha_up:
            if body < self.cfg.weak_body_atr_max or up_w > self.cfg.wick_ratio_trigger or comp > 0.25:
                return TrendState.UP_EXHAUST
            if (not bool(p["alpha_up"])) or (float(p["alpha_slope"]) <= 0 < alpha_slope):
                return TrendState.UP_START
            return TrendState.UP_TREND

        # Fast는 먼저 상승, Alpha는 아직 하락: 핵심 선행구간
        if fast_up and not alpha_up:
            if alpha_slope >= 0 or comp > 0.20 or low_w > self.cfg.wick_ratio_trigger:
                return TrendState.REVERSAL_ARMED
            return TrendState.DOWN_WEAKEN

        # 둘 다 하락
        if (not fast_up) and (not alpha_up):
            if body < self.cfg.weak_body_atr_max or low_w > self.cfg.wick_ratio_trigger or comp > 0.20:
                return TrendState.DOWN_WEAKEN
            return TrendState.DOWN

        # Fast 하락, Alpha 상승: 상승 중 단기 눌림/노이즈 가능
        if (not fast_up) and alpha_up:
            if alpha_slope > 0 and body < self.cfg.strong_body_atr_min:
                return TrendState.UP_TREND
            return TrendState.UP_EXHAUST

        return TrendState.DOWN_WEAKEN

    def _score(self, x: pd.DataFrame, state: TrendState) -> Tuple[float, Dict[str, float], List[str], List[str]]:
        r = x.iloc[-1]
        reasons: List[str] = []
        warnings: List[str] = []

        # 1) HA 구조 20
        ha = 0.0
        if r["fast_up"]:
            ha += 0.45
        if r["alpha_up"]:
            ha += 0.35
        if r["alpha_slope"] > 0:
            ha += 0.20
        ha = min(1.0, ha)

        # 2) 변곡/소진 20
        rev = 0.0
        comp = float(r["body_compression"])
        if comp > 0.10:
            rev += min(0.35, comp)
        if r["lower_wick_ratio"] >= self.cfg.wick_ratio_trigger:
            rev += 0.20
        if self.cfg.doji_body_atr_max >= r["body_atr"]:
            rev += 0.15
        if state == TrendState.REVERSAL_ARMED:
            rev += 0.35
        elif state == TrendState.UP_START:
            rev += 0.40
        elif state == TrendState.UP_TREND:
            rev += 0.20
        rev = min(1.0, rev)

        # 3) PRICE HOLD 15
        price = 0.0
        close = float(r["close"])
        atr = max(float(r["atr"]), 1e-12)
        vwap = float(r["vwap"])
        if close >= vwap:
            price += 0.45
        elif (vwap - close) <= self.cfg.vwap_tolerance_atr * atr:
            price += 0.25
        if close >= float(r["ema20"]):
            price += 0.35
        if float(r["ema10"]) >= float(r["ema20"]):
            price += 0.20
        price = min(1.0, price)

        # 4) RS 15
        rs_m = float(r["rs_market"])
        rs_s = float(r["rs_sector"])
        rs = 0.5 * _normalize_0_1(rs_m, -0.03, 0.05) + 0.5 * _normalize_0_1(rs_s, -0.03, 0.05)

        # 5) RVOL 10
        rvol = float(r["rvol"])
        vol = _normalize_0_1(rvol, 0.8, self.cfg.rvol_strong)

        # 6) 수급 10
        flow_z = float(r["flow_z"])
        flow = _normalize_0_1(flow_z, -1.0, 1.5)

        # 7) 섹터 10
        # sector_return 미제공이면 rs_sector를 proxy로 사용
        sec = _normalize_0_1(rs_s, -0.02, 0.04)
        if "sector_return" in x.columns:
            try:
                sr = float(pd.to_numeric(x["sector_return"], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0).iloc[-1])
                sec = 0.6 * sec + 0.4 * _normalize_0_1(sr, -0.02, 0.03)
            except Exception:
                pass

        parts = {
            "ha_structure": ha * self.cfg.w_ha_structure,
            "reversal": rev * self.cfg.w_reversal,
            "price_hold": price * self.cfg.w_price_hold,
            "rs": rs * self.cfg.w_rs,
            "volume": vol * self.cfg.w_volume,
            "flow": flow * self.cfg.w_flow,
            "sector": sec * self.cfg.w_sector,
        }
        score = float(sum(parts.values()))

        # 상태 보정
        if state == TrendState.UP_START:
            score += 5.0
            reasons.append("Fast HA와 Alpha HA가 상승 시작 구간")
        elif state == TrendState.REVERSAL_ARMED:
            score += 3.0
            reasons.append("Fast HA 선행전환 + Alpha HA 하락 둔화")
        elif state == TrendState.UP_TREND:
            score += 2.0
            reasons.append("상승 추세 유지")
        elif state == TrendState.UP_EXHAUST:
            score -= 7.0
            warnings.append("상승 소진 신호: 몸통 축소/상단꼬리/압축")
        elif state == TrendState.DOWN:
            score -= 8.0
            warnings.append("하락 추세 지속")

        # 과열/추격 감점
        if len(x) >= 2:
            daily_gain = (float(x["close"].iloc[-1]) / float(x["close"].iloc[-2]) - 1) * 100
            if daily_gain > self.cfg.max_preferred_daily_gain_pct:
                penalty = min(10.0, (daily_gain - self.cfg.max_preferred_daily_gain_pct) * 1.5)
                score -= penalty
                warnings.append(f"선행포착 관점에서 당일 상승률 {daily_gain:.2f}% 과열 감점")

        extension = (close - float(r["ema20"])) / atr
        if extension > self.cfg.price_extension_atr:
            score -= min(8.0, (extension - self.cfg.price_extension_atr) * 2.5)
            warnings.append("EMA20 대비 과도한 이격")

        # 설명
        if close >= vwap:
            reasons.append("실제가격 VWAP 상단 유지")
        if close >= float(r["ema20"]):
            reasons.append("EMA20 상단")
        if rvol >= self.cfg.rvol_strong:
            reasons.append(f"RVOL 강함({rvol:.2f})")
        if rs_m > 0:
            reasons.append("시장 대비 상대강도 양호")
        if rs_s > 0:
            reasons.append("업종 대비 상대강도 양호")
        if flow_z > 0.3:
            reasons.append("외인/기관/프로그램 수급 우호")
        if float(r["lower_wick_ratio"]) > self.cfg.wick_ratio_trigger:
            reasons.append("하단꼬리 증가: 매도압력 흡수 흔적")
        if float(r["body_compression"]) > 0.20:
            reasons.append("HA 몸통 압축: 변곡 준비 가능")

        return float(np.clip(score, 0, 100)), parts, reasons, warnings

    def _heuristic_probabilities(self, score: float, state: TrendState, rsi: float, rvol: float) -> Dict[int, float]:
        # 보정 전 규칙 기반 추정치. 절대 확률로 오해하지 않도록 probability_calibrated=False와 함께 반환.
        state_adj = {
            TrendState.DOWN: -0.75,
            TrendState.DOWN_WEAKEN: -0.20,
            TrendState.REVERSAL_ARMED: 0.20,
            TrendState.UP_START: 0.45,
            TrendState.UP_TREND: 0.30,
            TrendState.UP_EXHAUST: -0.10,
        }[state]

        rsi_adj = 0.0
        if 42 <= rsi <= 58:
            rsi_adj = 0.10
        elif rsi >= 72:
            rsi_adj = -0.12
        elif rsi <= 30:
            rsi_adj = 0.05

        vol_adj = 0.10 if rvol >= self.cfg.rvol_strong else (0.03 if rvol >= self.cfg.rvol_min else -0.05)

        base = (score - 55) / 13.0 + state_adj + rsi_adj + vol_adj
        p1 = _sigmoid(base - 0.15)
        p3 = _sigmoid(base + 0.05)
        p5 = _sigmoid(base + 0.10)

        # 지나친 자신감 제한
        return {
            1: float(np.clip(p1, 0.20, 0.82)),
            3: float(np.clip(p3, 0.22, 0.84)),
            5: float(np.clip(p5, 0.22, 0.85)),
        }

    def _lookup_calibrated_probability(self, state: TrendState, score: float, horizon: int) -> Optional[float]:
        if not self.calibration:
            return None

        bucket = int(min(95, max(0, math.floor(score / 5) * 5)))
        key = f"{bucket:02d}-{bucket+4:02d}"

        try:
            return float(self.calibration[state.value][key][horizon])
        except Exception:
            return None

    def analyze(self, df: pd.DataFrame, symbol: str = "UNKNOWN") -> SignalResult:
        x = self.enrich(df)
        state = self._state(x)
        score, parts, reasons, warnings = self._score(x, state)
        r = x.iloc[-1]

        if score >= self.cfg.score_top:
            grade = "S"
        elif score >= self.cfg.score_strong:
            grade = "A"
        elif score >= self.cfg.score_armed:
            grade = "B"
        elif score >= 60:
            grade = "WATCH"
        else:
            grade = "REJECT"

        heur = self._heuristic_probabilities(score, state, float(r["rsi"]), float(r["rvol"]))
        calibrated = False
        probs = {}
        for h in (1, 3, 5):
            cp = self._lookup_calibrated_probability(state, score, h)
            if cp is not None:
                probs[h] = cp
                calibrated = True
            else:
                probs[h] = heur[h]

        if bool(r["fast_up"]) and not bool(r["alpha_up"]):
            div = "FAST_LEADS_UP"
        elif (not bool(r["fast_up"])) and bool(r["alpha_up"]):
            div = "ALPHA_HOLDS_UP"
        elif bool(r["fast_up"]) and bool(r["alpha_up"]):
            div = "BOTH_UP"
        else:
            div = "BOTH_DOWN"

        return SignalResult(
            symbol=symbol,
            timestamp=r["timestamp"],
            state=state.value,
            score=round(score, 2),
            grade=grade,
            fast_ha_up=bool(r["fast_up"]),
            alpha_ha_up=bool(r["alpha_up"]),
            fast_alpha_divergence=div,
            body_atr=round(float(r["body_atr"]), 4),
            body_compression=round(float(r["body_compression"]), 4),
            lower_wick_ratio=round(float(r["lower_wick_ratio"]), 4),
            upper_wick_ratio=round(float(r["upper_wick_ratio"]), 4),
            close=round(float(r["close"]), 4),
            vwap=round(float(r["vwap"]), 4) if np.isfinite(float(r["vwap"])) else None,
            above_vwap=bool(float(r["close"]) >= float(r["vwap"])) if np.isfinite(float(r["vwap"])) else None,
            ema20_hold=bool(float(r["close"]) >= float(r["ema20"])),
            rsi=round(float(r["rsi"]), 2),
            rvol=round(float(r["rvol"]), 2),
            rs_market=round(float(r["rs_market"]), 4),
            rs_sector=round(float(r["rs_sector"]), 4),
            flow_score=round(parts["flow"], 2),
            sector_score=round(parts["sector"], 2),
            p_up_1d=round(probs[1] * 100, 1),
            p_up_3d=round(probs[3] * 100, 1),
            p_up_5d=round(probs[5] * 100, 1),
            probability_calibrated=calibrated,
            reasons=reasons,
            warnings=warnings,
        )

    # ---------- 전체시장 스캔 ----------
    def scan_universe(
        self,
        data_by_symbol: Mapping[str, pd.DataFrame],
        top_n: int = 20,
        allowed_states: Optional[Iterable[TrendState]] = None,
        min_score: float = 0.0,
    ) -> pd.DataFrame:
        allowed = set(s.value if isinstance(s, TrendState) else str(s) for s in allowed_states) if allowed_states else None
        rows = []

        for symbol, df in data_by_symbol.items():
            try:
                result = self.analyze(df, symbol=symbol)
                d = result.to_dict()
                if allowed is not None and d["state"] not in allowed:
                    continue
                if d["score"] < min_score:
                    continue
                rows.append(d)
            except Exception as e:
                rows.append({
                    "symbol": symbol,
                    "state": "ERROR",
                    "score": 0.0,
                    "grade": "ERROR",
                    "error": str(e),
                })

        if not rows:
            return pd.DataFrame()

        out = pd.DataFrame(rows)
        if "score" in out.columns:
            out = out.sort_values(["score", "p_up_5d"], ascending=[False, False], na_position="last")
        return out.head(top_n).reset_index(drop=True)

    # ---------- 경험적 확률 보정 ----------
    def calibrate_probabilities(
        self,
        historical_by_symbol: Mapping[str, pd.DataFrame],
        min_samples_per_bucket: int = 20,
        positive_threshold: float = 0.0,
    ) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, Dict[int, float]]]:
        """
        과거 각 시점에 대해 점수/상태를 만들고,
        1/3/5 거래일 후 수익률 > positive_threshold 인 비율을 경험적 확률로 저장.

        주의:
        - 충분한 히스토리가 필요함.
        - 정확한 확률모형이 아니라 '상태+점수 버킷별 empirical hit rate'.
        - 룩어헤드 방지를 위해 각 시점의 과거 데이터만으로 analyze() 수행.
        """
        records = []

        for symbol, raw in historical_by_symbol.items():
            x = self._validate(raw)
            if len(x) < 80:
                continue

            for i in range(60, len(x) - 5):
                hist = x.iloc[: i + 1].copy()
                try:
                    res = self.analyze(hist, symbol=symbol)
                except Exception:
                    continue

                close0 = float(x["close"].iloc[i])
                rec = {
                    "symbol": symbol,
                    "state": res.state,
                    "score": res.score,
                }
                for h in (1, 3, 5):
                    fut = float(x["close"].iloc[i + h])
                    rec[f"y{h}"] = 1 if (fut / close0 - 1) > positive_threshold else 0
                records.append(rec)

        if not records:
            raise ValueError("확률 보정에 사용할 충분한 과거 샘플이 없습니다.")

        r = pd.DataFrame(records)
        r["bucket"] = (np.floor(r["score"] / 5) * 5).clip(0, 95).astype(int)

        calibration: Dict[str, Dict[str, Dict[int, float]]] = {}
        for state, g_state in r.groupby("state"):
            calibration[state] = {}
            for bucket, g in g_state.groupby("bucket"):
                if len(g) < min_samples_per_bucket:
                    continue
                key = f"{bucket:02d}-{bucket+4:02d}"
                calibration[state][key] = {
                    1: float(g["y1"].mean()),
                    3: float(g["y3"].mean()),
                    5: float(g["y5"].mean()),
                }

        self.calibration = calibration
        return calibration


class MultiTimeframeEngine:
    """
    일봉 35% + 60분봉 35% + 15분봉 30% 결합.
    개별 엔진의 구조는 동일하게 유지하되, 실제 데이터 수집/리샘플링은 외부에서 수행 권장.
    """
    def __init__(
        self,
        engine: Optional[AlphaHeikinAshiEngine] = None,
        weights: Optional[Dict[str, float]] = None,
    ):
        self.engine = engine or AlphaHeikinAshiEngine()
        self.weights = weights or {"1D": 0.35, "60m": 0.35, "15m": 0.30}

    def analyze(
        self,
        symbol: str,
        daily: pd.DataFrame,
        m60: pd.DataFrame,
        m15: pd.DataFrame,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        results = {
            "1D": self.engine.analyze(daily, symbol),
            "60m": self.engine.analyze(m60, symbol),
            "15m": self.engine.analyze(m15, symbol),
        }

        weighted_score = 0.0
        weighted_p1 = 0.0
        weighted_p3 = 0.0
        weighted_p5 = 0.0

        for tf, res in results.items():
            w = self.weights[tf]
            weighted_score += res.score * w
            weighted_p1 += res.p_up_1d * w
            weighted_p3 += res.p_up_3d * w
            weighted_p5 += res.p_up_5d * w

        # 정렬성 보정
        states = [results[k].state for k in ("1D", "60m", "15m")]
        bullish = sum(s in {"REVERSAL_ARMED", "UP_START", "UP_TREND"} for s in states)

        alignment_bonus = 0.0
        if bullish == 3:
            alignment_bonus = 5.0
        elif bullish == 2:
            alignment_bonus = 2.0
        elif bullish == 0:
            alignment_bonus = -5.0

        final_score = float(np.clip(weighted_score + alignment_bonus, 0, 100))

        if final_score >= 88:
            grade = "S"
        elif final_score >= 80:
            grade = "A"
        elif final_score >= 70:
            grade = "B"
        elif final_score >= 60:
            grade = "WATCH"
        else:
            grade = "REJECT"

        # 실전 우선순위:
        # 일봉은 방향, 60분은 변곡, 15분은 진입 타이밍.
        if results["60m"].state in {"REVERSAL_ARMED", "UP_START"} and results["15m"].state in {"UP_START", "UP_TREND"}:
            final_state = "UP_START"
        elif bullish >= 2:
            final_state = "UP_TREND"
        elif results["1D"].state == "UP_EXHAUST":
            final_state = "UP_EXHAUST"
        elif results["60m"].state == "REVERSAL_ARMED":
            final_state = "REVERSAL_ARMED"
        else:
            final_state = results["60m"].state

        return {
            "symbol": symbol,
            "state": final_state,
            "score": round(final_score, 2),
            "grade": grade,
            "p_up_1d": round(weighted_p1, 1),
            "p_up_3d": round(weighted_p3, 1),
            "p_up_5d": round(weighted_p5, 1),
            "timeframes": {k: v.to_dict() for k, v in results.items()},
        }


def load_csv(path: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
    df = pd.read_csv(path)
    if "timestamp" in df.columns:
        # 가능한 경우 날짜/시간 파싱
        try:
            parsed = pd.to_datetime(df["timestamp"], errors="coerce")
            if parsed.notna().sum() > 0:
                df["timestamp"] = parsed
        except Exception:
            pass
    return df


def _demo():
    """
    외부 API 없이도 구조가 작동하는지 확인하는 간단 데모.
    실제 투자판정에는 실데이터를 사용해야 한다.
    """
    np.random.seed(7)
    n = 140
    base = 100 + np.cumsum(np.random.normal(0.05, 0.9, n))
    # 후반부 반전/상승 구조를 약하게 삽입
    base[-25:] += np.linspace(-2, 8, 25)

    close = pd.Series(base)
    open_ = close.shift(1).fillna(close.iloc[0]) + np.random.normal(0, 0.25, n)
    high = np.maximum(open_, close) + np.random.uniform(0.2, 1.0, n)
    low = np.minimum(open_, close) - np.random.uniform(0.2, 1.0, n)
    volume = np.random.lognormal(mean=12.0, sigma=0.35, size=n)
    volume[-10:] *= np.linspace(0.9, 1.5, 10)

    df = pd.DataFrame({
        "timestamp": pd.date_range("2026-01-01", periods=n, freq="B"),
        "open": open_,
        "high": high,
        "low": low,
        "close": close,
        "volume": volume,
        "foreign_net": np.random.normal(0, 1, n),
        "institution_net": np.random.normal(0, 1, n),
        "program_net": np.random.normal(0, 1, n),
        "market_return": np.random.normal(0.0002, 0.007, n),
        "sector_return": np.random.normal(0.0003, 0.009, n),
    })

    engine = AlphaHeikinAshiEngine()
    result = engine.analyze(df, symbol="DEMO")
    print(pd.Series(result.to_dict()).to_string())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    import argparse

    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="알파 하이킨아시 변곡예측 엔진")
    parser.add_argument("--csv", type=str, help="단일 종목 CSV 경로")
    parser.add_argument("--symbol", type=str, default="UNKNOWN", help="종목코드/이름")
    parser.add_argument("--demo", action="store_true", help="내장 데모 실행")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    if args.demo:
        _demo()
    elif args.csv:
        df = load_csv(args.csv)
        engine = AlphaHeikinAshiEngine()
        result = engine.analyze(df, symbol=args.symbol)
        print(pd.Series(result.to_dict()).to_string())
    else:
        parser.print_help()
